import { useRef } from 'react';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { useGSAP } from '@gsap/react';
import { ArrowRight, BookOpen, GitBranch, Layers, ShieldCheck } from 'lucide-react';
import evaluation from '../data/latest_eval_summary.json';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, useGSAP);

const PRINCIPLES = [
  { number: '01', title: 'Evidence stays visible', text: 'Returned sources carry their title, type, ID, excerpt, and link into the result.', Icon: BookOpen },
  { number: '02', title: 'Structured checks stay separate', text: 'Medication interactions come from the graph lookup and are shown separately from retrieved text.', Icon: GitBranch },
  { number: '03', title: 'Limits stay visible', text: 'Unavailable services, missing evidence, provisional labels, and blocked measurements remain explicit.', Icon: ShieldCheck },
];

function Metric({ label, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="info-principle">
      <div className="info-eyebrow">{label}</div>
      <p style={{ marginTop: '10px' }}>{children}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AboutPage({ onHowItWorks, onOpenLibrary, onEnterWorkspace }) {
  const root = useRef(null);

  const diagnosisMetric = evaluation.diagnosis_metric?.status === 'not_measured'
    ? 'Not measured'
    : `Top-1 ${evaluation.diagnosis_metric?.top1?.correct ?? 0}/${evaluation.diagnosis_metric?.top1?.n ?? 0}`;
  const controls = evaluation.controls || {};
  const interactionStatus = evaluation.interactions?.status || 'not measured';
  const runMode = evaluation.run_mode || 'unknown';

  useGSAP(
    () => {
      const mm = gsap.matchMedia();

      mm.add('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
        gsap.from('.info-hero h1, .info-hero .info-lead, .info-project-note', {
          y: 26,
          opacity: 0,
          stagger: 0.08,
          duration: 0.85,
          ease: 'expo.out',
        });

        gsap.utils.toArray('.info-section').forEach((section) => {
          gsap.from(section, {
            y: 28,
            opacity: 0,
            duration: 0.8,
            ease: 'expo.out',
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: section,
              start: 'top 85%',
              once: true,
            },
          });
        });

        gsap.utils.toArray('.info-principles').forEach((container) => {
          gsap.from(container.querySelectorAll('.info-principle'), {
            y: 24,
            opacity: 0,
            stagger: 0.08,
            duration: 0.75,
            ease: 'expo.out',
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: container,
              start: 'top 85%',
              once: true,
            },
          });
        });
      });

      return () => mm.revert();
    },
    { scope: root }
  );

  return (
    <main className="info-page" aria-labelledby="about-heading" ref={root}>
      <header className="info-hero">
        <div className="info-eyebrow"><span className="info-marker" /> THE PROJECT / 01</div>
        <div className="info-hero-grid">
          <div>
            <h1 id="about-heading">Clinical knowledge.<br /><em>Traceable results.</em></h1>
            <p className="info-lead">MedGraph combines validated intake, structured graph lookups, and indexed sources while showing what the system did and did not find.</p>
            <div className="info-actions">
              <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={onEnterWorkspace} data-cursor="OPEN">Explore the workspace <ArrowRight size={15} /></button>
              <a className="info-text-link" href="https://github.com/Akifali-1/MedGraph" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" data-cursor="OPEN"><GitBranch size={15} /> View the project</a>
            </div>
          </div>
          <aside className="info-project-note" aria-label="Project at a glance">
            <Layers size={25} strokeWidth={1.4} />
            <span className="info-eyebrow">IMPLEMENTED PATH</span>
            <p>Intake is checked.<br />Sources are retrieved.<br />Citations stay attached.</p>
            <dl className="info-facts">
              <div><dt>Graph</dt><dd>Neo4j</dd></div>
              <div><dt>Retrieval</dt><dd>Dense + BM25</dd></div>
              <div><dt>Default route</dt><dd>DAG</dd></div>
            </dl>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="info-section" aria-labelledby="does-heading">
        <div className="info-section-label">01 / WHAT IT DOES</div>
        <div className="info-section-body">
          <h2 id="does-heading">A grounded case <em>workspace.</em></h2>
          <p>MedGraph accepts age, sex, symptoms, medications, and comorbidities. It resolves common clinical terms, queries the graph for structured relationships, retrieves indexed guideline chunks, and returns a differential only when indexed guideline evidence is available.</p>
          <p>A medication interaction result is returned as a structured lookup. It is not silently converted into â€œno interactions foundâ€ when Neo4j is unavailable.</p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="info-section" aria-labelledby="works-heading">
        <div className="info-section-label">02 / HOW IT WORKS</div>
        <div className="info-section-body">
          <h2 id="works-heading">One request. Several <em>checks.</em></h2>
          <div className="info-pipeline" aria-label="Intake, normalization, graph and vector retrieval, citation filtering, and reasoning">
            <div><span className="info-eyebrow">01</span><strong>Intake + validation</strong><small>Schema limits and required fields</small></div>
            <ArrowRight size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
            <div><span className="info-eyebrow">02</span><strong>Normalize + retrieve</strong><small>Entity links, Neo4j, vector store, BM25</small></div>
            <ArrowRight size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
            <div><span className="info-eyebrow">03</span><strong>Filter + reason</strong><small>Metadata citations and response status</small></div>
          </div>
          <p>Gemini is the default reasoning model when configured. SageMaker is used only when `SAGEMAKER_ENDPOINT_NAME` is set; it is currently unset. The ReAct route remains opt-in; the default `/diagnose` route is the DAG path.</p>
          <p>If no retrieved chunk passes the retrieval boundary, the response is `no_evidence`, diagnoses is empty, and the exact message is shown. Graph interaction results are still returned. If the graph cannot be reached, the interaction check is marked unavailable.</p>
          <div className="info-actions">
            <button type="button" className="info-text-link" onClick={onHowItWorks} data-cursor="OPEN">Read a worked case <ArrowRight size={15} /></button>
            <button type="button" className="info-text-link" onClick={onOpenLibrary} data-cursor="OPEN">Browse the evidence library <ArrowRight size={15} /></button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="info-section" aria-labelledby="sources-heading">
        <div className="info-section-label">03 / DATA SOURCES</div>
        <div className="info-section-body">
          <h2 id="sources-heading">Attribution and <em>access.</em></h2>
          <div className="info-principles">
            <Metric label="Current interaction data">`synthetic-demo` â€” the current graph was not replaced because DDInter data was unavailable. Interaction alerts are labeled as demo data.</Metric>
            <Metric label="DDInter 2.0">Primary interaction candidate. CC BY-NC-SA 4.0; non-commercial redistribution with attribution and ShareAlike is allowed. Download/schema verification is blocked.</Metric>
            <Metric label="Columbia disease-symptom">Public source page describing 2004 discharge-summary data. No clear redistribution license was identified. The exposed disease count is documented as a source count; the edge score remains a labeled rank heuristic.</Metric>
            <Metric label="DrugBank + RxNav">DrugBank academic access is license-gated and was not used. The NLM RxNav interaction API was discontinued on January 2, 2024.</Metric>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="info-section" aria-labelledby="evaluation-heading">
        <div className="info-section-label">04 / EVALUATION</div>
        <div className="info-section-body">
          <h2 id="evaluation-heading">Measured boundaries. <em>Open gaps.</em></h2>
          <div className="info-principles">
            <Metric label="Diagnosis">{evaluation.diagnosis_cases} provisional cases; accuracy {diagnosisMetric}. The case set is underpowered versus the target of 40.</Metric>
            <Metric label="Controls">{controls.n ? `${controls.no_evidence ?? 0}/${controls.n} synthetic controls returned no_evidence.` : 'Not included in the latest diagnosis-only run.'}</Metric>
            <Metric label="Interactions">{interactionStatus}. Precision/recall and brand/synonym mapping were not measured.</Metric>
          </div>
          <p>Latest summary: <code>{evaluation.summary_path || 'eval/results/latest/summary.json'}</code>. This was a bounded {runMode} run; the provisional metrics do not establish clinical accuracy.</p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="info-section" aria-labelledby="limits-heading">
        <div className="info-section-label">05 / LIMITATIONS</div>
        <div className="info-section-body">
          <h2 id="limits-heading">What it does <em>not do.</em></h2>
          <p>MedGraph has not established clinical validity. The current diagnosis labels are provisional, the interaction source replacement is blocked, and the resumed Gemini evaluation included provider errors. Retrieved citations show the source chunk returned; they do not independently prove the cited claim.</p>
          <p className="info-footnote">MedGraph is a research and demonstration prototype, not medical advice. Do not use it as a substitute for a qualified clinician or emergency care.</p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <div className="info-principles" style={{ marginTop: '30px' }}>
        {PRINCIPLES.map(({ number, title, text, Icon }) => (
          <article className="info-principle" key={number}>
            <div className="info-card-top"><span className="info-eyebrow">{number}</span><Icon size={21} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
            <h3>{title}</h3>
            <p>{text}</p>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

import { 
  Stethoscope, 
  Mail, 
  Lock, 
  User, 
  UserCheck, 
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldAlert
} from 'lucide-react';

export default function AuthView({
  authMode,
  setAuthMode,
  role,
  setRole,
  emailInput,
  setEmailInput,
  passwordInput,
  setPasswordInput,
  nameInput,
  setNameInput,
  npiInput,
  setNpiInput,
  clinicInput,
  setClinicInput,
  handleLoginSubmit,
  handleSignupSubmit,
  authError,
  isGoogleSsoConfigured = false
}) {
  return (
    <main style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', flex: 1, padding: '60px 24px', position: 'relative' }}>
      {/* Background visual capsule glows */}
      <div 
        style={{ 
          position: 'absolute', 
          width: '320px', 
          height: '320px', 
          borderRadius: '50%', 
          background: role === 'doctor' ? 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.08) 0%, transparent 70%)' : 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.08) 0%, transparent 70%)',
          filter: 'blur(40px)',
          zIndex: -1,
          transition: 'background 0.5s ease'
        }}
      />
      
      <div 
        className="clinical-card" 
        style={{ 
          width: '100%', 
          maxWidth: '440px',
          boxShadow: role === 'doctor' 
            ? '0 24px 60px -15px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.15), var(--card-shadow)' 
            : '0 24px 60px -15px rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.15), var(--card-shadow)',
          borderColor: role === 'doctor'
            ? 'rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.25)'
            : 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.25)',
          transition: 'all 0.5s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)',
          padding: '36px'
        }}
      >
        
        {/* Role Selector Capsule */}
        <div className="workspace-tabs" style={{ marginBottom: '28px' }}>
                    <button 
            type="button"
            className={`workspace-tab-btn ${role === 'doctor' ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => setRole('doctor')}
            style={{ fontSize: '12.5px', padding: '10px 14px' }}
          >
            Practitioner
          </button>
                    <button 
            type="button"
            className={`workspace-tab-btn ${role === 'patient' ? 'active' : ''}`}
            onClick={() => setRole('patient')}
            style={{ fontSize: '12.5px', padding: '10px 14px' }}
          >
            Patient
          </button>
        </div>

        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: '28px' }}>
          <div 
            style={{ 
              width: '56px', 
              height: '56px', 
              borderRadius: '12px', 
              background: role === 'doctor' ? 'rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.06)' : 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.06)', 
              display: 'flex', 
              alignItems: 'center', 
              justifyContent: 'center', 
              margin: '0 auto 16px',
              border: role === 'doctor' ? '1px solid rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.2)' : '1px solid rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.2)',
              transition: 'all 0.5s ease'
            }}
          >
            <Stethoscope 
              size={24} 
              style={{ 
                color: role === 'doctor' ? 'var(--accent-cyan)' : 'var(--accent-purple)',
                filter: role === 'doctor' ? 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.4))' : 'drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.4))',
                transition: 'color 0.5s ease'
              }} 
            />
          </div>
          <h2 style={{ fontSize: '22px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>
            {role === 'doctor' 
              ? (authMode === 'login' ? 'Practitioner sign in' : 'Create practitioner account')
              : (authMode === 'login' ? 'Patient sign in' : 'Create patient account')}
          </h2>
          <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: '6px', lineHeight: '1.4' }}>
            {role === 'doctor'
              ? (authMode === 'login' ? 'Open the case workspace.' : 'Create your practitioner account.')
              : (authMode === 'login' ? 'Review your medicine safety checks.' : 'Create an account to save checks.')
            }
          </p>
        </div>

        {authError && (
          <div className="triage-banner warning" style={{ marginBottom: '18px', padding: '10px 12px' }}>
            <ShieldAlert size={14} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px' }}>{authError}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {authMode === 'login' ? (
          <form onSubmit={handleLoginSubmit}>
            <div className="form-group">
               <label className="form-label">Email</label>
              <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                <Mail size={16} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '14px', top: '14px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />

                  type="email"

                  value={emailInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmailInput(e.target.value)}
                  required 
                  style={{ paddingLeft: '44px' }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Password</label>
              <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                <Lock size={16} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '14px', top: '14px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
                <input 
                  type="password" 
                  className="form-input" 
                  placeholder="â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢â€¢" 
                  value={passwordInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setPasswordInput(e.target.value)}
                  required 
                  style={{ paddingLeft: '44px' }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {role === 'doctor' && (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', padding: '10px 12px', background: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.05)', border: '1px solid rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.15)', borderRadius: '8px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                <ShieldAlert size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-amber)', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Password sign-in is for local development.</span>
              </div>
            )}

            <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" style={{ width: '100%', marginTop: '10px' }}>
              Sign in
              <ArrowRight size={15} />
            </button>

            {/* Separator */}
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '20px 0', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', background: 'var(--panel-border)' }} />
              <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>or</span>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', background: 'var(--panel-border)' }} />
            </div>

            {isGoogleSsoConfigured ? (
              <div id="google-sso-button" style={{ width: '100%', minHeight: '40px', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center' }} />
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn btn-secondary"
                disabled
                style={{ width: '100%', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '8px', padding: '10px' }}
              >
                Google sign-in unavailable
              </button>
            )}

            <p style={{ textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: '24px' }}>
              New here?{' '}
              <span 
                style={{ color: role === 'doctor' ? 'var(--accent-cyan)' : 'var(--accent-purple)', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: '600' }}
                onClick={() => setAuthMode('signup')}
              >
                {role === 'doctor' ? 'Create account' : 'Create patient account'}
              </span>
            </p>
          </form>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSignupSubmit}>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Full name</label>
              <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                <User size={16} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '14px', top: '14px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
                <input 
                  type="text" 
                  className="form-input" 
                  placeholder={role === 'doctor' ? "Dr. Evelyn Carter, MD" : "Your full name"} 
                  value={nameInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setNameInput(e.target.value)}
                  required 
                  style={{ paddingLeft: '44px' }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {role === 'doctor' ? (

                <div>
                   <label className="form-label">NPI</label>

                    onChange={(e) => setNpiInput(e.target.value)}
                    required 
                  />

                <div>
                   <label className="form-label">Clinic</label>

                    type="text"

                    required 
                  />

              </div>

            <div className="form-group">

              <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                <Mail size={16} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '14px', top: '14px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />

                  type="email" 
                  className="form-input" 
                  placeholder="e.carter@medgraph.ai" 
                  value={emailInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmailInput(e.target.value)}
                  required 
                  style={{ paddingLeft: '44px' }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label">Password</label>
              <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
                <Lock size={16} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '14px', top: '14px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
                <input 
                  type="password" 
                  className="form-input" 
                  placeholder="Min. 8 characters" 
                  value={passwordInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setPasswordInput(e.target.value)}
                  required 
                  style={{ paddingLeft: '44px' }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" style={{ width: '100%', marginTop: '10px' }}>
              Confirm Account Setup
              <UserCheck size={15} />

            {/* Separator */}
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', margin: '20px 0', gap: '10px' }}>
              <div style={{ flex: 1, height: '1px', background: 'var(--panel-border)' }} />
              <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>or</span>

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\BrainNetwork.jsx =====

/**
 * BrainNetwork
 *
 * Hero figure: the brain-shaped node network, resting on a luminous platform.
 *
 * The brain is supplied artwork on a black field rather than an alpha channel,
 * so mix-blend-mode: screen drops the black - screen maps black to transparent
 * and preserves the bright cyan/violet network exactly, without the halo
 * fringing that pre-keying the PNG introduced.
 *
 * What remains here is the surrounding treatment: the platform it sits on, its
 * reflection, and a slow float so the figure reads as suspended rather than
 * pasted down. All motion is CSS (see cinematic.css) and transform/opacity
 * only, so there is no main-thread cost once settled and nothing to pause on
 * scroll.
 */

import brainSrc from '/brain-hero.png';

export default function BrainNetwork() {
  return (
    <div className="hero-figure" aria-hidden="true">
      <img className="hero-brain-img" src={brainSrc} alt="" draggable="false" />

      {/* Platform. Kept in normal flow after the artwork and pulled up with a
          negative margin, rather than absolutely positioned - the absolute
          version resolved against the wrong box and floated above the brain. */}
      <svg className="hero-plate-svg" viewBox="0 0 240 60" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet">
        <defs>
          <radialGradient id="hf-plate" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ffffff" stopOpacity="0.95" />
            <stop offset="55%" stopColor="#c9d9f6" stopOpacity="0.45" />
            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#c9d9f6" stopOpacity="0" />
          </radialGradient>
        </defs>
        <ellipse className="hero-plate" cx="120" cy="30" rx="82" ry="17" fill="url(#hf-plate)" />
        <ellipse className="hero-plate-ring" cx="120" cy="30" rx="62" ry="12" />
        <ellipse className="hero-plate-ring hero-plate-ring-2" cx="120" cy="30" rx="42" ry="8" />
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\CinematicLanding.jsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { useGSAP } from '@gsap/react';
import { ArrowRight, ArrowUpRight, MoveHorizontal } from 'lucide-react';
import HeroTelemetryCanvas from './HeroTelemetryCanvas';
import evaluation from '../data/latest_eval_summary.json';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, useGSAP);

const HERO_CASES = [
  {
    id: 'ddi',
    tab: '01 // DRUG CONFLICT HALT',
    tag: 'CASE 0417 Â· EARLY-EXIT INTERCEPT',
    tone: 'critical',
    bpm: '114 BPM Â· ALERT',
    xrayTag: 'INTERCEPT_LOCKED // RX-11289 Ã— RX-1191',
    nodes: [
      { step: '01 / INTAKE', ms: '014ms', title: 'Warfarin + Aspirin', meta: '65M Â· Chest pain Â· AFib' },
      { step: '02 / NEO4J GRAPH', ms: '048ms', title: '1-Hop DDI Edge', meta: 'RX-11289 â†” RX-1191 Â· HIGH' },
      { step: '03 / SAFETY GATE', ms: '061ms', title: 'Generation Halted', meta: 'route_after_graph: alert_early' },
      { step: '04 / SOURCE LOCK', ms: '112ms', title: 'AHA/ACC Â§10', meta: 'Major bleed risk Â· Chunk verified' },
    ],
    verdict: 'CRITICAL INTERACTION INTERCEPTED BEFORE LLM SYNTHESIS',
  },
  {

function StageSchematic({ activeStage, onSelectStage }) {
  const current = DISSECT_STAGES[activeStage] || DISSECT_STAGES[0];
  return (
    <div className="mgx-schematic-frame" data-stage={activeStage}>
      <div className="mgx-schematic-hud">
        <span>INSTRUMENT // VIEWPORT_0{activeStage + 1}</span>

          className={
            activeStage === 1
              ? 'is-alert'
              : activeStage === 3
                ? 'is-verified'
                : 'is-titanium'
          }
        >
          {activeStage === 1
            ? 'â— INTERCEPT ACTIVE'
            : activeStage === 3
              ? 'â— CITATIONS LOCKED'
              : 'â— DAG PIPELINE'}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="mgx-schematic-viewport">
        <svg
          className="mgx-schematic-svg"

            <text x="412" y="318" textAnchor="middle" className="mgx-svg-mono is-strong">DIAPHORESIS</text>

          </g>

          <g className={`mgx-svg-layer ${activeStage === 1 ? 'is-active' : ''}`}>

            <circle cx="110" cy="220" r="46" className="mgx-svg-core is-alert" />

            <rect x="354" y="150" width="120" height="40" className="mgx-svg-box is-verified" />
            <text x="414" y="174" textAnchor="middle" className="mgx-svg-mono is-strong">AHA/ACC Â§10</text>

            <rect x="354" y="250" width="120" height="40" className="mgx-svg-box is-verified" />
            <text x="414" y="274" textAnchor="middle" className="mgx-svg-mono is-strong">AHA 2021 Â§4.1</text>
          </g>

          {/* STAGE 3: Deterministic Citation Lock */}
          <g className={`mgx-svg-layer ${activeStage === 3 ? 'is-active' : ''}`}>
            <rect x="86" y="92" width="348" height="64" className="mgx-svg-box is-accent" />
            <text x="108" y="118" className="mgx-svg-mono is-strong">01 // MEDICATION INTERACTION HAZARD</text>
            <text x="108" y="138" className="mgx-svg-mono">CONFIDENCE 1.00  Â·  INTERCEPTED</text>

            <rect x="86" y="184" width="348" height="64" className="mgx-svg-box is-verified" />
            <text x="108" y="210" className="mgx-svg-mono is-strong">02 // ACUTE STEMI</text>
            <text x="108" y="230" className="mgx-svg-mono">CONFIDENCE 0.87  Â·  AHA/ACC 2021 LOCKED</text>

            <rect x="86" y="276" width="348" height="64" className="mgx-svg-box" />
            <text x="108" y="302" className="mgx-svg-mono is-strong">03 // UNSTABLE ANGINA</text>
            <text x="108" y="322" className="mgx-svg-mono">CONFIDENCE 0.71  Â·  AHA/ACC 2021 LOCKED</text>
          </g>
        </svg>
      </div>

      <div className="mgx-schematic-footer">
        <div className="mgx-stage-pills" role="tablist" aria-label="Pipeline stage jump">
          {DISSECT_STAGES.map((st, idx) => (
            <button
              key={st.num}
              type="button"
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={activeStage === idx}
              className={`mgx-stage-pill ${activeStage === idx ? 'is-active' : ''}`}
              onClick={() => onSelectStage(idx)}

            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <span
          className={
            current.isCritical ? 'is-alert' : current.isVerified ? 'is-verified' : 'is-titanium'
          }
        >
          {current.badge}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function CinematicLanding({
  isLoggedIn,
  onEnterWorkspace,
  onLogin,
  onRegister,
  onHowItWorks,
}) {
  const root = useRef(null);
  const heroPinRef = useRef(null);
  const traceDeckRef = useRef(null);
  const loupeRingRef = useRef(null);
  const coordReadoutRef = useRef(null);
  const splitBoxRef = useRef(null);
  const marqueeTrackRef = useRef(null);
  const beatRefs = useRef([]);

  const [heroCaseIdx, setHeroCaseIdx] = useState(0);
  const [activeStep, setActiveStep] = useState(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) {
      return 2;
    }
    return 0;
  });
  const activeHeroCase = HERO_CASES[heroCaseIdx] || HERO_CASES[0];
  const [activeStage, setActiveStage] = useState(0);
  const [splitPct, setSplitPct] = useState(48);
  const [isDraggingSplit, setIsDraggingSplit] = useState(false);

  const citationRate =
    evaluation.citation_resolution?.rate != null
      ? `${Math.round(evaluation.citation_resolution.rate * 100)}%`
      : 'N/A';
  const citationCount = `${evaluation.citation_resolution?.resolved ?? 0}/${evaluation.citation_resolution?.total ?? 0}`;
  const latencyP50 = evaluation.diagnosis_latency_ms?.p50
    ? `${(evaluation.diagnosis_latency_ms.p50 / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`
    : 'N/A';
  const evalCases = `${evaluation.diagnosis_cases_evaluated ?? 0}/${evaluation.diagnosis_cases ?? 0}`;
  const interactionStatus = String(evaluation.interactions?.status || 'demo').toUpperCase();

  // 1. Live Self-Executing DAG Sequencer:
  //    Steps 01 -> 02 -> 03 -> 04, then auto-advances to the next tab and loops.
  useEffect(() => {
    const prefersReduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (prefersReduced) return undefined;

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (activeStep < 3) {
        setActiveStep((prev) => prev + 1);
      } else {
        setActiveStep(0);
        setHeroCaseIdx((prev) => (prev + 1) % HERO_CASES.length);
      }
    }, 1350);

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [heroCaseIdx, activeStep]);

  // 2. 60fps Diagnostic X-Ray Loupe:
  //    - 20% smaller radius (110px)
  //    - Expands ONLY when hovering directly over the headline text spans
  //    - Clips cleanly above .mgx-trace-deck so it never interferes with the bottom deck
  useEffect(() => {
    const heroEl = heroPinRef.current;
    if (!heroEl) return undefined;
    if (window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) return undefined;
    if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return undefined;

    const LOUPE_RADIUS = 110; // 20% smaller than 138px
    let tx = heroEl.clientWidth * 0.35;
    let ty = heroEl.clientHeight * 0.34;
    let cx = tx;
    let cy = ty;
    let tr = 0;
    let cr = 0;
    let rafId = 0;

    const syncBounds = () => {
      const heroH = heroEl.clientHeight;
      heroEl.style.setProperty('--hero-h', `${heroH}px`);
      const deckEl = traceDeckRef.current;
      if (deckEl) {
        const deckTop = deckEl.offsetTop;
        const cutBottom = Math.max(0, heroH - deckTop + 6);
        heroEl.style.setProperty('--xray-cut-bottom', `${cutBottom}px`);
      }
    };

    syncBounds();
    window.addEventListener('resize', syncBounds);

    const onMove = (e) => {
      const rect = heroEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      const inside =
        e.clientX >= rect.left &&
        e.clientX <= rect.right &&
        e.clientY >= rect.top &&
        e.clientY <= rect.bottom;

      if (coordReadoutRef.current) {
        const sx = String(Math.max(0, Math.round(e.clientX))).padStart(4, '0');
        const sy = String(Math.max(0, Math.round(e.clientY))).padStart(4, '0');
        coordReadoutRef.current.textContent = `X:${sx}  Y:${sy}`;
      }

      if (!inside) {
        tr = 0;
        return;
      }

      tx = e.clientX - rect.left;
      ty = e.clientY - rect.top;

      // Expand ONLY when hovering directly over the actual headline text
      const overHeadlineText = Boolean(e.target?.closest?.('.mgx-hero-line-inner'));
      tr = overHeadlineText ? LOUPE_RADIUS : 0;
    };

    const onLeave = () => {
      tr = 0;
    };

    const tick = () => {
      cx += (tx - cx) * 0.18;
      cy += (ty - cy) * 0.18;
      cr += (tr - cr) * 0.16;

      heroEl.style.setProperty('--lx', `${cx.toFixed(1)}px`);
      heroEl.style.setProperty('--ly', `${cy.toFixed(1)}px`);
      heroEl.style.setProperty('--lr', `${cr.toFixed(1)}px`);

      if (loupeRingRef.current) {
        const scale = cr / LOUPE_RADIUS;
        loupeRingRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${cx.toFixed(1)}px, ${cy.toFixed(1)}px, 0) scale(${scale.toFixed(3)})`;
        loupeRingRef.current.style.opacity = cr > 6 ? '1' : '0';
      }

      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true });
    heroEl.addEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      window.removeEventListener('resize', syncBounds);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      heroEl.removeEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);
    };
  }, []);

  const updateSplitFromClientX = (clientX) => {
    const box = splitBoxRef.current;
    if (!box) return;
    const rect = box.getBoundingClientRect();
    const raw = ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;
    setSplitPct(Math.min(92, Math.max(8, raw)));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isDraggingSplit) return undefined;
    const onMove = (e) => {
      const x = e.touches ? e.touches[0].clientX : e.clientX;
      updateSplitFromClientX(x);
    };
    const onUp = () => setIsDraggingSplit(false);

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
    window.addEventListener('touchmove', onMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
    window.addEventListener('touchend', onUp);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      window.removeEventListener('touchmove', onMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
      window.removeEventListener('touchend', onUp);
    };
  }, [isDraggingSplit]);

  const jumpToStage = (idx) => {
    setActiveStage(idx);
    const target = beatRefs.current[idx];
    if (target) {
      target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
    }
  };

  useGSAP(
    () => {
      const mm = gsap.matchMedia();

      mm.add('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
        // Crisp entrance that never traps elements invisible
        gsap.from('.mgx-hero-line-inner', {
          yPercent: 100,
          duration: 0.9,
          stagger: 0.08,
          ease: 'expo.out',
          clearProps: 'transform',
        });

        gsap.from('.mgx-hero-fade', {
          y: 16,
          opacity: 0,
          duration: 0.75,
          stagger: 0.06,
          delay: 0.15,
          ease: 'expo.out',
          clearProps: 'transform,opacity',
        });

        // Accurate Stage Tracking across all 4 chapters
        gsap.utils.toArray('.mgx-stage-beat').forEach((beat, idx) => {
          ScrollTrigger.create({
            trigger: beat,
            start: 'top 52%',
            end: 'bottom 52%',
            onEnter: () => setActiveStage(idx),
            onEnterBack: () => setActiveStage(idx),
          });
        });

        // Velocity-reactive ticker
        if (marqueeTrackRef.current) {
          const marqueeTween = gsap.to(marqueeTrackRef.current, {
            xPercent: -50,
            repeat: -1,
            duration: 24,
            ease: 'none',
          });

          ScrollTrigger.create({
            trigger: '.mgx-marquee',
            start: 'top bottom',
            end: 'bottom top',
            onUpdate: (self) => {
              const vel = Math.abs(self.getVelocity()) / 420;
              const dir = self.direction === -1 ? -1 : 1;
              gsap.to(marqueeTween, {
                timeScale: dir * (1 + Math.min(vel, 4.5)),
                duration: 0.3,
                overwrite: true,
              });
            },
          });
        }

        gsap.utils.toArray('.mgx-eval-bar-fill').forEach((bar) => {
          gsap.fromTo(
            bar,
            { scaleX: 0 },
            {
              scaleX: parseFloat(bar.dataset.fill || '1'),
              duration: 1.0,
              ease: 'expo.out',
              scrollTrigger: {
                trigger: bar,
                start: 'top 88%',
                once: true,
              },
            }
          );
        });

        gsap.utils.toArray('[data-mgx-reveal]').forEach((el) => {
          gsap.from(el, {
            y: 34,
            opacity: 0,
            duration: 0.85,
            ease: 'expo.out',
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: el,
              start: 'top 85%',
              once: true,
            },
          });
        });
      });

      // Desktop-Only Hero Pin + Bulletproof Pipeline Instrument Pin
      mm.add('(min-width: 1025px) and (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
        // 1. Pinned Hero -> Restored Expanding Light-White Archival Aperture
        const heroTl = gsap.timeline({
          scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: '.mgx-hero-pin',
            start: 'top top',
            end: '+=135%',
            pin: true,
            scrub: 0.75,
            invalidateOnRefresh: true,
          },
        });

        heroTl
          .to(
            '.mgx-hero-content',
            {
              scale: 0.94,
              y: -28,
              opacity: 0.16,
              ease: 'none',
              immediateRender: false,
            },
            0
          )
          .fromTo(
            '.mgx-aperture',
            {
              clipPath: 'inset(42% 30% 42% 30%)',
              opacity: 0,
            },
            {
              clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)',
              opacity: 1,
              ease: 'power2.inOut',
              duration: 0.62,
              immediateRender: false,
            },
            0.18
          )
          .fromTo(
            '.mgx-aperture-word',
            { yPercent: 85, opacity: 0 },
            {
              yPercent: 0,
              opacity: 1,
              stagger: 0.04,
              duration: 0.34,
              ease: 'expo.out',
              immediateRender: false,
            },
            0.46
          );

        // 2. Bulletproof GSAP Pin for the Left Pipeline Instrument Box
        //    Works reliably even when body has overflow-x: hidden
        ScrollTrigger.create({
          trigger: '.mgx-dissect-grid',
          pin: '.mgx-dissect-sticky',
          start: 'top 94px',
          end: () => {
            const grid = root.current?.querySelector('.mgx-dissect-grid');
            const sticky = root.current?.querySelector('.mgx-dissect-sticky');
            if (!grid || !sticky) return 'bottom bottom';
            const travel = Math.max(0, grid.offsetHeight - sticky.offsetHeight);
            return `+=${travel}`;
          },
          pinSpacing: false,
          invalidateOnRefresh: true,
        });
      });

      return () => mm.revert();
    },
    { scope: root }

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\ClinicalCursor.jsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

export default function ClinicalCursor() {
  const cursorRef = useRef(null);
  const [label, setLabel] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    if (window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) return;
    if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;

    let mx = -120;
    let my = -120;
    let cx = -120;
    let cy = -120;
    let rafId = 0;

    const onMove = (e) => {
      mx = e.clientX;
      my = e.clientY;
      const target = e.target?.closest?.('[data-cursor]');
      setLabel(target ? target.getAttribute('data-cursor') || '' : '');
    };

    const tick = () => {
      cx += (mx - cx) * 0.18;
      cy += (my - cy) * 0.18;
      if (cursorRef.current) {
        cursorRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${cx}px, ${cy}px, 0)`;
      }
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true });
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      ref={cursorRef}
      className="mg-cursor"
      data-mode={label ? 'label' : 'dot'}
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <div className="mg-cursor-dot">
        {label ? <span className="mg-cursor-text">{label}</span> : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\ClinicalFieldCanvas.jsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

/**
 * ClinicalFieldCanvas
 * Full-bleed 60fps interactive 3D clinical topology.
 * - Sweeping tilted dual-strand clinical ribbon across 100vw
 * - Real-time cursor probe reticle with triangulation chords + entity lock
 * - Interactive DDI Halt shockwave morph (haltMode = true)
 * - True Z-axis camera fly-through driven by scroll progressRef (0 -> 1)
 * Zero gradients, zero WebGL bundle overhead.
 */
const CLINICAL_TAGS = [
  { label: 'WARFARIN', code: 'RX-11289', kind: 'critical' },
  { label: 'ASPIRIN', code: 'RX-1191', kind: 'critical' },
  { label: 'CHEST PAIN', code: 'UMLS-C0008031', kind: 'titanium' },
  { label: 'DIAPHORESIS', code: 'UMLS-C0700590', kind: 'titanium' },
  { label: 'ACUTE STEMI', code: 'ICD-I21.3', kind: 'critical' },
  { label: 'AHA/ACC Â§10', code: 'CHUNK VERIFIED', kind: 'verified' },
  { label: 'ACC/AHA Â§4.1', code: 'ECG 10-MIN', kind: 'verified' },
  { label: 'NEO4J 1-HOP', code: 'DDI SEVERITY: HI', kind: 'critical' },
];

export default function ClinicalFieldCanvas({ progressRef, haltMode = false }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const haltRef = useRef(haltMode);
  const shockRef = useRef(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (haltRef.current !== haltMode) {
      haltRef.current = haltMode;
      shockRef.current = 1; // Trigger crisp geometric shockwave ring
    }
  }, [haltMode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return undefined;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const prefersReduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    let rafId = 0;
    let width = 0;
    let height = 0;
    let isVisible = true;
    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);

    const COUNT = 320;
    const nodes = Array.from({ length: COUNT }, (_, i) => {
      const t = i / COUNT;
      const strand = i % 2; // 0 = Ontology Ribbon, 1 = Pharmacology Ribbon
      const angle = t * Math.PI * 6 + strand * Math.PI;
      const spreadX = (t - 0.5) * 920;
      const radius = 115 + Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * 95 + (i % 7) * 7;

      // State A: Sweeping Sculptural Double-Helix / Clinical Ribbon (tilted across viewport)
      const rawX = spreadX;
      const rawY = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
      const rawZ = Math.sin(angle) * radius;

      // Tilt -20deg in XY plane so it sweeps dramatically between diagonal headlines
      const tilt = -0.34;
      const sFlow = {
        x: rawX * Math.cos(tilt) - rawY * Math.sin(tilt),
        y: rawX * Math.sin(tilt) + rawY * Math.cos(tilt),
        z: rawZ,
      };

      // State B: DDI Halt Mode â€” Two Opposed Pharmacological Planes (Warfarin Left / Aspirin Right)
      const side = strand === 0 ? -1 : 1;
      const row = Math.floor((i / 2) % 16) - 7.5;
      const col = Math.floor(i / 32) - 4.5;
      const sHalt = {
        x: side * (215 + Math.abs(col) * 14),
        y: row * 28,
        z: col * 38,
      };

      return {
        sFlow,
        sHalt,
        strand,
        phase: (i * 0.37) % (Math.PI * 2),
        isCritical: i % 17 === 0,
        isVerified: i % 19 === 0 && i % 17 !== 0,
        tag: CLINICAL_TAGS[i % CLINICAL_TAGS.length],
      };
    });

    // Traveling signal packets along the ribbon
    const pulses = Array.from({ length: 18 }, (_, i) => ({
      pos: (i / 18) * COUNT,
      speed: 0.22 + (i % 4) * 0.08,
      critical: i % 3 === 0,
    }));

    const pointer = {
      x: -999,
      y: -999,
      tx: -999,
      ty: -999,
      active: false,
      nx: 0,
      ny: 0,
    };

    const getPalette = () => {
      const isLight = document.documentElement.classList.contains('light-mode');
      return isLight
        ? {
            ink: '12, 12, 11',
            titanium: '#8d6c3a',
            accent: '#b8292f',
            verified: '#1f6346',
            tagBg: 'rgba(242, 239, 233, 0.94)',
            tagInk: '#0c0c0b',
          }
        : {
            ink: '240, 237, 230',
            titanium: '#c8b288',
            accent: '#ec463e',
            verified: '#45c898',
            tagBg: 'rgba(6, 6, 8, 0.92)',
            tagInk: '#f0ede6',
          };
    };

    const resize = () => {
      width = canvas.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
      height = canvas.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
      canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };

    const onMove = (e) => {
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const inside =
        e.clientX >= rect.left &&
        e.clientX <= rect.right &&
        e.clientY >= rect.top &&
        e.clientY <= rect.bottom;
      pointer.active = inside;
      if (inside) {
        pointer.tx = e.clientX - rect.left;
        pointer.ty = e.clientY - rect.top;
        pointer.nx = (pointer.tx / width - 0.5) * 2;
        pointer.ny = (pointer.ty / height - 0.5) * 2;
      }
    };

    const onLeave = () => {
      pointer.active = false;
    };

    const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
    let time = 0;
    let haltBlend = haltRef.current ? 1 : 0;
    let camRotX = 0;
    let camRotY = 0;

    const drawFrame = () => {
      if (!prefersReduced) time += 0.0055;

      // Smooth blend between Flow Ribbon (0) and DDI Halt Wall (1)
      const targetHalt = haltRef.current ? 1 : 0;
      haltBlend += (targetHalt - haltBlend) * 0.065;

      // Shockwave ring decay
      if (shockRef.current > 0.005) {
        shockRef.current *= 0.92;
      } else {
        shockRef.current = 0;
      }

      // Smooth pointer tracking
      if (pointer.active) {
        if (pointer.x < -500) {
          pointer.x = pointer.tx;
          pointer.y = pointer.ty;
        } else {
          pointer.x += (pointer.tx - pointer.x) * 0.16;
          pointer.y += (pointer.ty - pointer.y) * 0.16;
        }
      }

      camRotY += (pointer.nx * 0.34 - camRotY) * 0.045;
      camRotX += (pointer.ny * 0.24 - camRotX) * 0.045;

      const scrollP = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, progressRef?.current ?? 0));
      const palette = getPalette();

      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      const cx = width * 0.5;
      const cy = height * 0.5;
      const scaleW = Math.min(1.25, Math.max(0.62, width / 1280));

      // True Z-axis camera dive as user scrolls (camera pushes into the 3D field)
      const cameraZ = 640 - scrollP * 470;
      const fov = 560;

      // Architectural background calibration lines (ultra-subtle 1px)
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${palette.ink}, 0.055)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(cx, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(cx, height);
      ctx.moveTo(0, cy);
      ctx.lineTo(width, cy);
      ctx.stroke();

      // Expanding crisp shockwave ring on Halt toggle
      if (shockRef.current > 0.01) {
        const ringR = (1 - shockRef.current) * Math.max(width, height) * 0.72;
        ctx.strokeStyle = palette.accent;
        ctx.globalAlpha = shockRef.current * 0.85;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(cx, cy, ringR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();

      const rotY = time * 0.42 + camRotY + scrollP * 0.9;
      const rotX = Math.sin(time * 0.35) * 0.1 + camRotX;
      const cosY = Math.cos(rotY);
      const sinY = Math.sin(rotY);
      const cosX = Math.cos(rotX);
      const sinX = Math.sin(rotX);

      const projected = new Array(COUNT);
      const nearCursor = [];
      const probeRadius = 150;

      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        const n = nodes[i];
        const wave = Math.sin(time * 1.4 + n.phase) * 9 * (1 - haltBlend);

        const bx = lerp(n.sFlow.x, n.sHalt.x, haltBlend) * scaleW;
        const by = (lerp(n.sFlow.y, n.sHalt.y, haltBlend) + wave) * scaleW;
        const bz = lerp(n.sFlow.z, n.sHalt.z, haltBlend) * scaleW;

        const x1 = bx * cosY - bz * sinY;
        const z1 = bx * sinY + bz * cosY;
        const y2 = by * cosX - z1 * sinX;
        const z2 = by * sinX + z1 * cosX;

        const depth = z2 + cameraZ;
        if (depth < 40) {
          projected[i] = null;
          continue;
        }

        const scale = fov / depth;
        let sx = cx + x1 * scale;
        let sy = cy + y2 * scale;

        // Magnetic pointer deflection inside Probe Reticle
        let distToCursor = 9999;
        if (pointer.active && scrollP < 0.35) {
          const dx = sx - pointer.x;
          const dy = sy - pointer.y;
          distToCursor = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          if (distToCursor < probeRadius && distToCursor > 1) {
            const push = (1 - distToCursor / probeRadius) * 22;
            sx += (dx / distToCursor) * push;
            sy += (dy / distToCursor) * push;
          }
        }

        const pt = {
          sx,
          sy,
          scale,
          depth,
          strand: n.strand,
          isCritical: n.isCritical,
          isVerified: n.isVerified,
          distToCursor,
          tag: n.tag,
        };
        projected[i] = pt;

        if (distToCursor < probeRadius) {
          nearCursor.push(pt);
        }
      }

      // 1. Draw structural spine + cross-strand clinical edges
      ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;
      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        const a = projected[i];
        if (!a) continue;

        // Spine neighbor along same strand
        const nextSame = projected[i + 2];
        if (nextSame) {
          const dist = Math.hypot(a.sx - nextSame.sx, a.sy - nextSame.sy);
          if (dist < 140) {
            const alpha = Math.min(0.42, (1 - dist / 140) * 0.38 * a.scale);
            ctx.strokeStyle =
              haltBlend > 0.5 && a.strand === 0
                ? palette.accent
                : `rgba(${palette.ink}, ${alpha.toFixed(3)})`;
            ctx.globalAlpha = haltBlend > 0.5 && a.strand === 0 ? Math.min(0.55, alpha * 1.6) : 1;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(a.sx, a.sy);
            ctx.lineTo(nextSame.sx, nextSame.sy);
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }

        // Cross-strand rungs (turning into tense vermilion fault lines in Halt Mode)
        if (i % 4 === 0 && i + 1 < COUNT) {
          const b = projected[i + 1];
          if (b) {
            const isHot = haltBlend > 0.35 || a.isCritical;
            ctx.strokeStyle = isHot
              ? palette.accent
              : `rgba(${palette.ink}, ${(0.14 * Math.min(1, a.scale)).toFixed(3)})`;
            ctx.globalAlpha = isHot ? Math.min(0.75, 0.25 + haltBlend * 0.5) : 1;
            ctx.lineWidth = isHot && haltBlend > 0.5 ? 1.1 : 0.65;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(a.sx, a.sy);
            ctx.lineTo(b.sx, b.sy);
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.lineWidth = 0.75;
          }
        }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

      // 2. Draw crisp nodes
      for (let i = 0; i < COUNT; i++) {
        const pt = projected[i];
        if (!pt) continue;

        const inProbe = pt.distToCursor < probeRadius;
        const baseR = pt.isCritical ? 3.1 : pt.isVerified ? 2.4 : 1.55;
        const r = Math.max(1.0, (inProbe ? baseR * 1.45 : baseR) * Math.min(1.8, pt.scale));
        const alpha = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.14, pt.scale * 0.85));

        if (pt.isCritical || (haltBlend > 0.6 && pt.strand === 0 && i % 5 === 0)) {
          ctx.fillStyle = palette.accent;
          ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, alpha + 0.15);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(pt.sx, pt.sy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        } else if (pt.isVerified) {
          ctx.fillStyle = palette.verified;
          ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(pt.sx, pt.sy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        } else {
          ctx.fillStyle = inProbe
            ? palette.titanium
            : `rgba(${palette.ink}, ${(alpha * 0.72).toFixed(3)})`;
          ctx.globalAlpha = inProbe ? 1 : 1;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(pt.sx, pt.sy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.fill();
        }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

      // 3. Traveling live clinical signal packets
      if (!prefersReduced) {
        for (const p of pulses) {
          p.pos = (p.pos + p.speed * (haltBlend > 0.5 ? 0.25 : 1)) % (COUNT - 2);
          const idx = Math.floor(p.pos);
          const frac = p.pos - idx;
          const a = projected[idx];
          const b = projected[idx + 2];
          if (!a || !b) continue;
          const px = lerp(a.sx, b.sx, frac);
          const py = lerp(a.sy, b.sy, frac);
          const pr = Math.max(1.8, 3.2 * Math.min(1.6, a.scale));
          ctx.fillStyle = p.critical ? palette.accent : palette.verified;
          ctx.fillRect(px - pr * 0.5, py - pr * 0.5, pr, pr);
        }
      }

      // 4. Center Halt Fault Banner (only visible when user triggers Simulate DDI Halt)
      if (haltBlend > 0.45 && scrollP < 0.25) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = (haltBlend - 0.45) / 0.55;
        ctx.strokeStyle = palette.accent;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx - 210 * scaleW, cy);
        ctx.lineTo(cx + 210 * scaleW, cy);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.setLineDash([]);

        const bw = 270;
        const bh = 38;
        ctx.fillStyle = palette.accent;
        ctx.fillRect(cx - bw / 2, cy - bh / 2, bw, bh);

        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.font = '600 10px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.fillText('INTERCEPTED // WARFARIN Ã— ASPIRIN', cx, cy - 2);
        ctx.font = '400 8.5px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
        ctx.fillText('SEVERITY: HIGH Â· GENERATION HALTED', cx, cy + 12);
        ctx.restore();
      }

      // 5. Interactive Cursor Probe Reticle + Live Triangulation Chords
      if (pointer.active && scrollP < 0.3 && width >= 900) {
        nearCursor.sort((a, b) => a.distToCursor - b.distToCursor);
        const locked = nearCursor.slice(0, 6);

        ctx.save();
        // Precision crosshair reticle ring
        ctx.strokeStyle = haltBlend > 0.5 ? palette.accent : palette.titanium;
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.5;
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(pointer.x, pointer.y, 44, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();

        // Triangulation chords from cursor to nearest live 3D nodes
        locked.forEach((pt, idx) => {
          ctx.strokeStyle =
            pt.isCritical || haltBlend > 0.5
              ? palette.accent
              : pt.isVerified
                ? palette.verified
                : palette.titanium;
          ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0.18, 0.72 - idx * 0.1);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(pointer.x, pointer.y);
          ctx.lineTo(pt.sx, pt.sy);
          ctx.stroke();
        });

        // Live Entity HUD readout anchored to closest locked node
        const closest = locked[0];
        if (closest) {
          const rx = Math.min(width - 178, pointer.x + 56);
          const ry = Math.max(24, Math.min(height - 54, pointer.y - 18));
          const borderCol =
            closest.tag.kind === 'critical'
              ? palette.accent
              : closest.tag.kind === 'verified'
                ? palette.verified
                : palette.titanium;

          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.96;
          ctx.fillStyle = palette.tagBg;
          ctx.fillRect(rx, ry, 154, 34);
          ctx.strokeStyle = borderCol;
          ctx.lineWidth = 1;
          ctx.strokeRect(rx, ry, 154, 34);

          ctx.fillStyle = palette.tagInk;
          ctx.font = '600 9.5px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
          ctx.textAlign = 'left';
          ctx.fillText(closest.tag.label, rx + 9, ry + 14);

          ctx.fillStyle = borderCol;
          ctx.font = '400 8.5px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
          ctx.fillText(closest.tag.code, rx + 9, ry + 26);
        }
        ctx.restore();
      }
    };

    const loop = () => {
      if (isVisible) drawFrame();
      if (!prefersReduced) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };

    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        isVisible = entry.isIntersecting;
      },
      { threshold: 0 }
    );
    io.observe(canvas);

    resize();
    if (prefersReduced) {
      drawFrame();
    } else {
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    }

    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      io.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseleave', onLeave);
    };
  }, [progressRef]);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="mgx-field-canvas" aria-hidden="true" />;
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\ContactPage.jsx =====

import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { useGSAP } from '@gsap/react';
import { ArrowRight, Check, ChevronDown, Copy, ExternalLink, GitBranch, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, useGSAP);

const FAQS = [
  { question: 'Where should I report a bug?', answer: 'Choose â€œBug reportâ€ and include what you searched for, what you expected, and what happened.' },
  { question: 'Can I suggest a source?', answer: 'Yes. Choose â€œEvidence or datasetâ€ and include the publisher and a link.' },
  { question: 'How do I run the project locally?', answer: 'The README has the setup steps and local server commands.' },
];

export default function ContactPage() {
  const root = useRef(null);
  const [topic, setTopic] = useState('Bug report');
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState('');
  const [details, setDetails] = useState('');
  const [prepared, setPrepared] = useState(false);
  const [copyState, setCopyState] = useState('');
  const body = `## ${topic}\n\n${details.trim()}\n\n---\nPrepared from the MedGraph project contact page.`;
  const issueUrl = `https://github.com/Akifali-1/MedGraph/issues/new?${new URLSearchParams({ title: `[${topic}] ${summary.trim()}`, body })}`;

  const prepareIssue = event => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setPrepared(true);
    setCopyState('');
  };

  const copyDraft = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(`${summary.trim()}\n\n${body}`);
      setCopyState('copied');
    } catch {
      setCopyState('error');
    }
  };

  useGSAP(
    () => {
      const mm = gsap.matchMedia();

      mm.add('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
        gsap.from('.info-hero h1, .info-hero .info-lead', {
          y: 24,
          opacity: 0,
          stagger: 0.08,
          duration: 0.8,
          ease: 'expo.out',
        });

        gsap.from('.info-contact-form', {
          y: 28,
          opacity: 0,
          duration: 0.85,
          ease: 'expo.out',
          delay: 0.1,
        });

        gsap.from('.info-contact-aside', {
          y: 28,
          opacity: 0,
          duration: 0.85,
          ease: 'expo.out',
          delay: 0.2,
        });
      });

      return () => mm.revert();
    },
    { scope: root }
  );

  return (
    <main className="info-page" aria-labelledby="contact-heading" ref={root}>
      <header className="info-hero info-hero-compact">
        <div className="info-eyebrow"><span className="info-marker" /> OPEN A CONVERSATION / 03</div>
        <h1 id="contact-heading">Better questions.<br /><em>A better MedGraph.</em></h1>
        <p className="info-lead">Found a bug or have an idea? Send a clear note to help improve the project.</p>
      </header>

      <div className="info-contact-grid">
        <section className="info-contact-form" aria-labelledby="feedback-heading">
          <div className="info-card-top"><span className="info-eyebrow">PROJECT FEEDBACK</span><MessageSquare size={20} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
          <h2 id="feedback-heading">Write a project <em>note.</em></h2>
          <p>Prepare a note here, then open it in GitHub.</p>
          <form onSubmit={prepareIssue} onChange={() => { setPrepared(false); setCopyState(''); }}>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label" htmlFor="contact-topic">What would you like to discuss?</label>
              <select className="form-select" id="contact-topic" value={topic} onChange={event => setTopic(event.target.value)}>
                <option>Bug report</option><option>Feature idea</option><option>Evidence or dataset</option><option>Project question</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label" htmlFor="contact-summary">Short summary</label>
              <input className="form-input" id="contact-summary" required minLength={5} maxLength={120} placeholder="e.g. An unrelated guideline appears for an empty search" value={summary} onChange={event => setSummary(event.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label className="form-label" htmlFor="contact-details">Details and steps to reproduce</label>
              <textarea className="form-input info-textarea" id="contact-details" required minLength={15} maxLength={1800} rows={6} placeholder="What did you try? What did you expect? What happened instead?" value={details} onChange={event => setDetails(event.target.value)} />
              <span className="info-field-note">{details.length} / 1800 characters Â· Use example data when describing a case.</span>
            </div>
            <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" data-cursor="OPEN">Prepare GitHub issue <ArrowRight size={15} /></button>
          </form>

          {prepared && (
            <div className="info-draft" role="status">
              <span className="info-eyebrow"><Check size={14} /> DRAFT READY</span>
              <p>Your draft is ready. Review it on GitHub before submitting.</p>
              <div className="info-actions">
                <a className="btn btn-primary" href={issueUrl} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" data-cursor="OPEN">Open issue draft <ExternalLink size={14} /></a>
                <button type="button" className="btn btn-secondary" onClick={copyDraft} data-cursor="OPEN"><Copy size={14} /> {copyState === 'copied' ? 'Copied' : 'Copy draft'}</button>
              </div>
              {copyState === 'error' && <p>Clipboard access is unavailable. You can still open the issue draft on GitHub.</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        <aside className="info-contact-aside">
          <span className="info-eyebrow">BUILT IN THE OPEN</span>
          <h2>Start with <em>the code.</em></h2>
          <p>Explore the code, see open issues, or contribute a change.</p>
          <a className="info-text-link" href="https://github.com/Akifali-1/MedGraph" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" data-cursor="OPEN"><GitBranch size={16} /> Project repository <ExternalLink size={13} /></a>
          <a className="info-text-link" href="https://github.com/Akifali-1/MedGraph/issues" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" data-cursor="OPEN"><MessageSquare size={16} /> Browse existing issues <ExternalLink size={13} /></a>
          <div className="info-faq">
            <span className="info-eyebrow">QUICK ANSWERS</span>
            {FAQS.map(({ question, answer }) => (
              <details key={question}><summary>{question}<ChevronDown size={16} aria-hidden="true" /></summary><p>{answer}</p></details>
            ))}
          </div>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
