import { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { useGSAP } from '@gsap/react';
import { ArrowRight, BookOpen, ExternalLink, Search, X } from 'lucide-react';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, useGSAP);

const SOURCES = [
  { id: 'chest-pain', type: 'Guidelines', publisher: 'AHA / ACC', year: '2021', title: 'Chest pain guidance', description: 'A reference for first steps when someone has acute or stable chest pain.', topics: ['Chest pain', 'Cardiology', 'ECG'], href: 'https://www.ahajournals.org/doi/10.1161/CIR.0000000000001029' },
  { id: 'aortic-disease', type: 'Guidelines', publisher: 'ACC / AHA', year: '2022', title: 'Aortic disease guidance', description: 'A reference for aortic disease, risk factors, imaging, and urgent care.', topics: ['Aortic disease', 'Imaging', 'Cardiology'], href: 'https://www.ahajournals.org/doi/10.1161/CIR.0000000000001106' },
  { id: 'antithrombotic', type: 'Literature', publisher: 'PubMed', year: 'Research index', title: 'Warfarin and aspirin', description: 'Research on combined blood thinners and bleeding risk.', topics: ['Warfarin', 'Aspirin', 'Drug interactions'], href: 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/?term=warfarin+aspirin+bleeding+risk' },
  { id: 'pubmed', type: 'Literature', publisher: 'National Library of Medicine', year: 'Research index', title: 'PubMed literature', description: 'Search biomedical papers and open the original abstracts.', topics: ['Biomedical research', 'Abstracts', 'Publications'], href: 'https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/' },
  { id: 'rxnav', type: 'Knowledge graph', publisher: 'National Library of Medicine', year: 'Terminology resource', title: 'RxNav drug names', description: 'Drug names and concepts used to connect medicines to structured knowledge.', topics: ['RxNorm', 'Medications', 'Terminology'], href: 'https://lhncbc.nlm.nih.gov/RxNav/' },
  { id: 'project-data', type: 'Knowledge graph', publisher: 'MedGraph', year: 'Project data', title: 'Graph seed data', description: 'Starter drug-interaction and symptom-disease data used by the project.', topics: ['Neo4j', 'Graph seeds', 'Open source'], href: 'https://github.com/Akifali-1/MedGraph/tree/main/backend/graph' },
];

const FILTERS = ['All sources', 'Guidelines', 'Literature', 'Knowledge graph'];

export default function EvidenceLibraryPage({ onEnterWorkspace }) {
  const root = useRef(null);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('All sources');
  const search = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const sources = SOURCES.filter(source => (
    (filter === 'All sources' || source.type === filter)
    && `${source.title} ${source.description} ${source.publisher} ${source.topics.join(' ')}`.toLowerCase().includes(search)
  ));

  const resetFilters = () => { setQuery(''); setFilter('All sources'); };

  useGSAP(
    () => {
      const mm = gsap.matchMedia();

      mm.add('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
        gsap.from('.info-hero h1, .info-hero .info-lead', {
          y: 24,
          opacity: 0,
          stagger: 0.08,
          duration: 0.8,
          ease: 'expo.out',
        });

        gsap.from('.info-source-card', {
          y: 24,
          opacity: 0,
          stagger: 0.07,
          duration: 0.7,
          ease: 'expo.out',
          scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: '.info-source-grid',
            start: 'top 85%',
            once: true,
          },
        });
      });

      return () => mm.revert();
    },
    { scope: root, dependencies: [filter, search] }
  );

  return (
    <main className="info-page" aria-labelledby="library-heading" ref={root}>
      <header className="info-hero info-hero-compact">
        <div className="info-eyebrow"><span className="info-marker" /> REFERENCE DESK / 02</div>
        <h1 id="library-heading">Go back to <em>the source.</em></h1>
        <p className="info-lead">Browse the guidelines, papers, and structured data behind MedGraph.</p>
      </header>

      <section className="info-library" aria-labelledby="sources-heading">
        <h2 id="sources-heading" className="info-visually-hidden">Browse reference sources</h2>
        <div className="info-library-toolbar">
          <div className="info-search">
            <Search size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
            <input type="search" aria-label="Search evidence sources" placeholder="Search a topic, drug, or publisherâ€¦" value={query} onChange={event => setQuery(event.target.value)} />
            {query && <button type="button" aria-label="Clear search" onClick={() => setQuery('')}><X size={16} /></button>}
          </div>
          <span className="info-result-count" role="status">{sources.length} {sources.length === 1 ? 'source' : 'sources'}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="info-filters" role="group" aria-label="Filter sources by type">
          {FILTERS.map(item => (
            <button type="button" key={item} className={`info-filter ${filter === item ? 'is-active' : ''}`} aria-pressed={filter === item} onClick={() => setFilter(item)}>{item}</button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {sources.length ? (
          <div className="info-source-grid">
            {sources.map(source => (
              <article className="info-source-card" key={source.id} data-cursor="SOURCE">
                <div className="info-card-top"><span className="info-source-type">{source.type}</span><BookOpen size={18} strokeWidth={1.5} aria-hidden="true" /></div>
                <h3>{source.title}</h3>
                <p>{source.description}</p>
                <div className="info-topic-list">{source.topics.map(topic => <span key={topic}>{topic}</span>)}</div>
                <div className="info-source-footer">
                  <span>{source.publisher}<small>{source.year}</small></span>
                  <a className="info-text-link" href={source.href} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label={`Open ${source.title} in a new tab`} data-cursor="OPEN">Open source <ExternalLink size={14} /></a>
                </div>
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="info-no-results">
            <Search size={30} strokeWidth={1.3} aria-hidden="true" />
            <h3>No matching sources found.</h3>
            <p>Try a broader topic such as â€œcardiologyâ€ or â€œmedicationsâ€, or clear the filters.</p>
            <button type="button" className="btn btn-secondary" onClick={resetFilters}>Clear search and filters</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <aside className="info-callout">
        <div><span className="info-eyebrow">ADD A SOURCE</span><h2>Bring your own <em>knowledge.</em></h2><p>Practitioners can add documents from the Uploads tab in the workspace.</p></div>
        <button type="button" className="btn btn-primary" onClick={onEnterWorkspace} data-cursor="OPEN">Open workspace <ArrowRight size={15} /></button>
      </aside>
      <p className="info-footnote">This is a curated reference catalog. These links are not patient-specific retrieval results, and listing a source does not mean the full publication is indexed in MedGraph.</p>
    </main>
  );
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\HeroTelemetryCanvas.jsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

/**
 * HeroTelemetryCanvas
 * Full-bleed 60fps clinical ECG oscilloscope + live graph topology layer.
 * Reacts dynamically to `tone`:
 *   - 'critical': P-QRS-T cardiac trace hitting a vermilion DDI intercept gate
 *   - 'verified': Authentic ST-elevation (STEMI) trace with emerald citation bursts
 *   - 'titanium': Calm precision calibration baseline (no_evidence boundary)
 * Zero gradients; uses stepped alpha vector trails and crisp 1px geometry.
 */
export default function HeroTelemetryCanvas({ tone = 'critical' }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const toneRef = useRef(tone);

  useEffect(() => {
    toneRef.current = tone;
  }, [tone]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return undefined;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const prefersReduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    let rafId = 0;
    let width = 0;
    let height = 0;
    let isVisible = true;
    const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);

    // Floating clinical graph nodes suspended around the waveform band
    const GRAPH_COUNT = 34;
    const graphNodes = Array.from({ length: GRAPH_COUNT }, (_, i) => ({
      rx: (i + 0.5) / GRAPH_COUNT + ((i * 19) % 10 - 5) * 0.008,
      ry: 0.24 + (((i * 37) % 100) / 100) * 0.48,
      vx: ((i % 2 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * (0.00004 + (i % 5) * 0.000012)),
      vy: ((i % 3 === 0 ? 1 : -1) * (0.00005 + (i % 4) * 0.000015)),
      r: i % 5 === 0 ? 2.8 : 1.5,
      key: i % 5 === 0,
    }));

    const pointer = { nx: 0, ny: 0, tx: 0, ty: 0 };

    const getPalette = () => {
      const isLight = document.documentElement.classList.contains('light-mode');
      return isLight
        ? {
            ink: '12, 12, 11',
            critical: '#b8292f',
            verified: '#1f6346',
            titanium: '#8d6c3a',
          }
        : {
            ink: '240, 237, 230',
            critical: '#ec463e',
            verified: '#45c898',
            titanium: '#c8b288',
          };
    };

    // Synthesize realistic ECG waveform amplitude for phase p in [0, 1)
    const sampleWave = (p, mode, t) => {
      if (mode === 'titanium') {
        // Calm calibration micro-pulse for no_evidence control
        const gatePulse = Math.abs(p - 0.5) < 0.025 ? Math.sin(((p - 0.475) / 0.05) * Math.PI) * 0.22 : 0;
        return Math.sin(p * Math.PI * 4 + t * 2) * 0.03 + gatePulse;
      }

      // Standard P wave
      const pWave = 0.14 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.18) / 0.032, 2));
      // Q dip
      const qDip = -0.18 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.295) / 0.011, 2));
      // Sharp R spike
      const rPeak = 1.0 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.32) / 0.012, 2));
      // S dip
      const sDip = -0.32 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.348) / 0.013, 2));

      if (mode === 'verified') {
        // ST-Elevation (STEMI) tombstone elevation + broad T wave
        const stElev = 0.36 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.43) / 0.075, 2));
        const tWave = 0.28 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.54) / 0.055, 2));
        return pWave + qDip + rPeak + sDip + stElev + tWave;
      }

      // Critical DDI mode: sharp ectopic spike + tension jitter after R-peak
      const tWave = 0.24 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.52) / 0.048, 2));
      const ddiSpike =
        p > 0.62 && p < 0.72
          ? Math.sin((p - 0.62) * 95 + t * 14) * 0.26 * Math.exp(-Math.pow((p - 0.67) / 0.035, 2))
          : 0;
      return pWave + qDip + rPeak + sDip + tWave + ddiSpike;
    };

    const resize = () => {
      width = canvas.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
      height = canvas.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
      canvas.width = Math.floor(width * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.floor(height * dpr);
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };

    const onMove = (e) => {
      pointer.tx = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
      pointer.ty = (e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
    };

    let time = 0;
    let scanX = 0;

    const drawFrame = () => {
      if (!prefersReduced) {
        time += 0.016;
        scanX = (scanX + Math.max(2.4, width * 0.0028)) % (width || 1);
      } else {
        scanX = width * 0.64;
      }

      pointer.nx += (pointer.tx - pointer.nx) * 0.05;
      pointer.ny += (pointer.ty - pointer.ny) * 0.05;

      const mode = toneRef.current;
      const pal = getPalette();
      const activeColor =
        mode === 'verified' ? pal.verified : mode === 'titanium' ? pal.titanium : pal.critical;

      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      const baselineY = height * 0.615 + pointer.ny * 5;
      const amp = Math.min(58, height * 0.092);
      const cyclePx = Math.max(240, Math.min(360, width * 0.27));

      // 1. Subtle drifting knowledge-graph mesh behind waveform
      const pxCoords = graphNodes.map((n) => {
        if (!prefersReduced) {
          n.rx += n.vx;
          n.ry += n.vy;
          if (n.rx < 0.03 || n.rx > 0.97) n.vx *= -1;
          if (n.ry < 0.14 || n.ry > 0.78) n.vy *= -1;
        }
        return {
          x: n.rx * width + pointer.nx * (n.key ? 18 : 9),
          y: n.ry * height + pointer.ny * (n.key ? 14 : 7),
          r: n.r,
          key: n.key,
        };
      });

      ctx.lineWidth = 0.7;
      for (let i = 0; i < pxCoords.length; i++) {
        const a = pxCoords[i];
        for (let j = i + 1; j < pxCoords.length; j++) {
          const b = pxCoords[j];
          const d = Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
          if (d < 165) {
            const alpha = (1 - d / 165) * 0.13;
            ctx.strokeStyle =
              a.key && b.key
                ? activeColor
                : `rgba(${pal.ink}, ${alpha.toFixed(3)})`;
            ctx.globalAlpha = a.key && b.key ? Math.min(0.32, alpha * 2.2) : 1;
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y);
            ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

      for (let i = 0; i < pxCoords.length; i++) {
        const pt = pxCoords[i];
        const nearScan = Math.abs(pt.x - scanX) < 110;
        ctx.fillStyle = pt.key || nearScan ? activeColor : `rgba(${pal.ink}, 0.28)`;
        ctx.globalAlpha = nearScan ? 0.95 : pt.key ? 0.55 : 1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, nearScan ? pt.r * 1.45 : pt.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        if (nearScan && pt.key) {
          // Live vertical telemetry tether down to the ECG baseline
          ctx.strokeStyle = activeColor;
          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
          ctx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(pt.x, pt.y);
          ctx.lineTo(pt.x, baselineY);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.setLineDash([]);
        }
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

      // 2. Horizontal Clinical Calibration Axis + Millisecond Ticks
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${pal.ink}, 0.1)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, baselineY);
      ctx.lineTo(width, baselineY);
      ctx.stroke();

      const tickStep = 48;
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let x = 0; x < width; x += tickStep) {
        const tall = x % (tickStep * 4) === 0 ? 7 : 3;
        ctx.moveTo(x, baselineY - tall);
        ctx.lineTo(x, baselineY + tall);
      }
      ctx.stroke();

      // 3. Real-Time Sweeping ECG Oscilloscope Trace (Stepped Alpha Trail, Zero Gradients)
      const step = 3;
      const tailLen = Math.min(480, width * 0.42);

      // Ghost trace (full width faint reference)
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${pal.ink}, 0.1)`;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (let x = 0; x <= width; x += step) {
        const phase = (((x + time * 14) % cyclePx) + cyclePx) % cyclePx / cyclePx;
        const y = baselineY - sampleWave(phase, mode, time) * amp;
        if (x === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();

      // Active Phosphor Sweep Segments trailing behind scanX
      const segments = 14;
      const segPx = tailLen / segments;
      for (let s = 0; s < segments; s++) {
        const distFromHead = s * segPx;
        const xEnd = scanX - distFromHead;
        const xStart = xEnd - segPx - step;
        const alpha = Math.pow(1 - s / segments, 1.5) * 0.92;

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.strokeStyle = activeColor;
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
        ctx.lineWidth = s < 3 ? 2.2 : 1.45;

        let started = false;
        for (let x = Math.floor(xStart); x <= Math.ceil(xEnd); x += step) {
          const wx = ((x % width) + width) % width;
          // Break stroke across wrap seam
          if (started && Math.abs(wx - (((x - step) % width) + width) % width) > step * 3) {
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.beginPath();
            started = false;
          }
          const phase = (((wx + time * 14) % cyclePx) + cyclePx) % cyclePx / cyclePx;
          const y = baselineY - sampleWave(phase, mode, time) * amp;
          if (!started) {
            ctx.moveTo(wx, y);
            started = true;
          } else {
            ctx.lineTo(wx, y);
          }
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

      // 4. Crisp Vertical Scan Beam + Active Head Dot
      const headPhase = (((scanX + time * 14) % cyclePx) + cyclePx) % cyclePx / cyclePx;
      const headY = baselineY - sampleWave(headPhase, mode, time) * amp;

      ctx.strokeStyle = activeColor;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.22;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(scanX, 0);
      ctx.lineTo(scanX, height);
      ctx.stroke();

      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = activeColor;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(scanX, headY, 3.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      ctx.strokeStyle = activeColor;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.45;
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(scanX, headY, 10, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    };

    const loop = () => {
      if (isVisible) drawFrame();
      if (!prefersReduced) rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };

    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        isVisible = entry.isIntersecting;
      },
      { threshold: 0 }
    );
    io.observe(canvas);

    resize();
    if (prefersReduced) drawFrame();
    else rafId = requestAnimationFrame(loop);

    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true });

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
      io.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('resize', resize);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
    };
  }, []);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="mgx-telemetry-canvas" aria-hidden="true" />;
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\HowItWorks.jsx =====

import { useRef } from 'react';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { useGSAP } from '@gsap/react';
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, useGSAP);

/**
 * How MedGraph Works â€” the case document.
 *
 * A long-form explainer that works a single clinical case in front of the reader
 * rather than describing the product in feature blocks. The case is the real one the
 * backend ships as its canonical test: 65M on warfarin and aspirin presenting
 * with chest pain. The drug interaction short-circuits the pipeline before the
 * model is consulted (see backend/agents/orchestrator.py route_after_graph), so
 * the document short-circuits too.
 *
 * Motion is GSAP throughout; the console uses Motion. One library per tree.
 * All of it runs inside gsap.matchMedia() keyed on prefers-reduced-motion, and
 * the CSS carries finished-state fallbacks so nothing depends on JS to be legible.
 */

const DIFFERENTIALS = [
  { name: 'Medication interaction hazard', src: 'RxNav Â· AHA/ACC antithrombotic', score: 1.0 },
  { name: 'STEMI', src: 'AHA/ACC 2021 Â· guideline 1, 3', score: 0.87 },
  { name: 'Unstable angina', src: 'AHA/ACC 2021 Â· guideline 1, 4', score: 0.71 },
  { name: 'Aortic dissection', src: 'AHA/ACC 2022 Â· guideline 6, 8', score: 0.34 },
];

export default function HowItWorks({ isLoggedIn, onEnterWorkspace, onLogin, onRegister }) {
  const root = useRef(null);

  useGSAP(
    () => {
      const mm = gsap.matchMedia();

      mm.add('(prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)', () => {
        /* Reading progress */
        gsap.to('.doc-progress', {
          scaleX: 1,
          ease: 'none',
          scrollTrigger: { start: 0, end: 'max', scrub: 0.3 },
        });

        /* Masthead and intake settle on load */
        gsap
          .timeline({ defaults: { ease: 'power3.out' } })
          .from('.doc-masthead', { y: 16, opacity: 0, duration: 0.7 })
          .from('.doc-intake dd, .doc-intake dt', { opacity: 0, duration: 0.5, stagger: 0.05 }, '-=0.35');

        /* Entity resolution: the patient's words get struck and rewritten
           in clinical vocabulary as the reader reaches them. */
        gsap.utils.toArray('.term').forEach((term) => {
          const strike = term.querySelector('.term-strike');
          const canon = term.querySelector('.term-canon');
          gsap
            .timeline({ scrollTrigger: { trigger: term, start: 'top 78%', once: true } })
            .to(strike, { scaleX: 1, duration: 0.35, ease: 'power2.inOut' })
            .to(canon, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.3, ease: 'power2.out' }, '-=0.1');
        });

        /* Margin notes: subtle scrubbed parallax */
        gsap.utils.toArray('.doc-note').forEach((note) => {
          gsap.to(note, {
            y: -18,
            ease: 'none',
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: note,
              start: 'top bottom',
              end: 'bottom top',
              scrub: 0.8,
            },
          });
        });

        /* Prose movements rise as they enter */
        gsap.utils.toArray('[data-rise]').forEach((el) => {
          gsap.from(el, {
            y: 20,
            opacity: 0,
            duration: 0.65,
            ease: 'power3.out',
            scrollTrigger: { trigger: el, start: 'top 86%', once: true },
          });
        });

        /* The halt. The rule draws across, then the trace prints line by line. */
        gsap
          .timeline({ scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.doc-halt', start: 'top 68%', once: true } })
          .to('.doc-halt-rule', { scaleX: 1, duration: 0.7, ease: 'power3.inOut' })
          .from('.doc-halt-tag', { opacity: 0, duration: 0.3 }, '-=0.3')
          .from('.doc-halt h2', { y: 24, opacity: 0, duration: 0.7 }, '-=0.15')
          .from('.doc-halt-drug', { opacity: 0, y: 10, duration: 0.4, stagger: 0.12 }, '-=0.35')
          .from('.doc-halt-verdict', { opacity: 0, duration: 0.4 }, '-=0.1')
          .from('.doc-halt-body', { opacity: 0, y: 12, duration: 0.5 }, '-=0.25')
          .from('.doc-halt-trace span', { opacity: 0, duration: 0.25, stagger: 0.09 }, '-=0.2');

        /* Confidence bars draw to their score */
        gsap.utils.toArray('.dx-bar i').forEach((bar) => {
          gsap.to(bar, {
            scaleX: bar.dataset.score,
            duration: 0.9,
            ease: 'power3.out',
            scrollTrigger: { trigger: bar, start: 'top 90%', once: true },
          });
        });

        /* Differential rows settle in order */
        gsap.from('.doc-dx tbody tr', {
          opacity: 0,
          y: 14,
          duration: 0.5,
          stagger: 0.08,
          ease: 'power3.out',
          scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.doc-dx', start: 'top 82%', once: true },
        });
      });

      return () => mm.revert();
    },
    { scope: root },
  );

  return (
    <main className="doc" ref={root}>
      <div className="doc-progress" />

      {/* ---------- Masthead ---------- */}
      <div className="doc-page">
        <div className="doc-margin" />
        <div className="doc-body">
          <header className="doc-masthead">
            <div className="doc-masthead-row">
              <h1>
                A case, <em>worked</em>
              </h1>
              <span className="doc-stamp">MedGraph Â· case 0417</span>
            </div>
          </header>

          {/* ---------- Intake ---------- */}
          <section className="doc-intake">
            <dl>
              <dt>Patient</dt>
              <dd>65, male</dd>

              <dt>Presenting</dt>
              <dd>
                <span className="term">
                  <span className="term-raw">
                    chest pressure
                    <span className="term-strike" />
                  </span>
                  <span className="term-canon">chest pain</span>
                </span>
                , jaw pain,{' '}
                <span className="term">
                  <span className="term-raw">
                    sweating
                    <span className="term-strike" />
                  </span>
                  <span className="term-canon">diaphoresis</span>
                </span>
              </dd>

              <dt>Medications</dt>
              <dd>Warfarin 5mg daily, Aspirin 81mg daily</dd>

              <dt>History</dt>
              <dd>Atrial fibrillation, hypertension</dd>
            </dl>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ---------- Movement 1: resolution ---------- */}
      <div className="doc-page">
        <aside className="doc-margin">
          <div className="doc-note doc-sticky">
            <span className="doc-note-label">Names checked</span>
            Everyday words are matched to the terms used by the graph.
            <cite>entity_linker.py</cite>
          </div>
        </aside>
        <div className="doc-body">
          <section className="doc-move" data-rise>
            <h2>Patients use everyday words</h2>
            <p>
              A patient may say â€œchest pressureâ€ or â€œsweatingâ€. The system matches those
              words to the terms used in its medical sources.
            </p>
            <p>This first step keeps the rest of the case on the right track.</p>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ---------- Movement 2: the graph ---------- */}
      <div className="doc-page">
        <aside className="doc-margin">
          <div className="doc-note doc-sticky">
            <span className="doc-note-label">Query</span>
            <span style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)', fontSize: '0.6875rem', lineHeight: 1.8 }}>
              MATCH (a:Drug)-[r:INTERACTS_WITH]-&gt;(b:Drug)
              <br />
              WHERE a.name IN $meds
              <br />
              RETURN r.severity
            </span>
            <cite>graph/client.py</cite>
          </div>
        </aside>
        <div className="doc-body">
          <section className="doc-move" data-rise>
            <h2>
              Two medicines, <em>one important link</em>
            </h2>
            <p>
              The medication list is checked before any generated answer. Warfarin and
              aspirin are both present, with a known high-risk link between them.
            </p>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ---------- The halt ---------- */}
      <section className="doc-halt">
        <div className="doc-halt-inner">
          <div className="doc-halt-rule" />
          <span className="doc-halt-tag">Safety stop</span>
          <h2>
            The check stops <em>here</em>
          </h2>

          <div className="doc-halt-pair">
            <span className="doc-halt-drug">Warfarin</span>
            <span className="doc-halt-op">+</span>
            <span className="doc-halt-drug">Aspirin</span>
            <span className="doc-halt-verdict">Severity: high</span>
          </div>

          <p className="doc-halt-body">
            Warfarin and aspirin together can raise bleeding risk. Because the conflict
            is already known, the app shows an alert before generating more text.
          </p>

          <div className="doc-halt-trace">
            <span className="ok">resolve_clinical_entities ok 4 terms</span>
            <span className="ok">query_knowledge_graph ok 1 interaction</span>
            <span className="stop">critical_interaction_flag true</span>
            <span className="stop">route_after_graph alert_early</span>
            <span>source lookup continued</span>
            <span>extra generation skipped</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ---------- Movement 3: evidence ---------- */}
      <div className="doc-page">
        <aside className="doc-margin">
          <div className="doc-note doc-sticky">
            <span className="doc-note-label">Source</span>
            <span className="doc-note-quote">
              Concurrent anticoagulant and antiplatelet therapy substantially increases
              the risk of major haemorrhage and should be limited to patients with a
              clear indication.
            </span>
            <cite>AHA/ACC Â· guideline 10</cite>
          </div>
        </aside>
        <div className="doc-body">
          <section className="doc-move" data-rise>
            <h2>Every claim carries its source</h2>
            <p>
              The alert includes a guideline reference. Each citation is checked against
              the source text used for the case.
            </p>
            <p>
              If a reference cannot be checked, it is left out of the report.
            </p>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ---------- Differential ---------- */}
      <div className="doc-page">
        <aside className="doc-margin">
          <div className="doc-note doc-sticky">
            <span className="doc-note-label">Note</span>
            The likely cardiac findings stay visible for review.
          </div>
        </aside>
        <div className="doc-body">
          <section className="doc-move" data-rise>
            <h2>What comes back</h2>
            <table className="doc-dx">
              <caption>Ranked differential Â· case 0417</caption>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th scope="col">Finding</th>
                  <th scope="col">Confidence</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {DIFFERENTIALS.map((d) => (
                  <tr key={d.name}>
                    <td>
                      <span className="dx-name">{d.name}</span>
                      <span className="dx-src">{d.src}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                      <span className="dx-score">{d.score.toFixed(2)}</span>
                      <span className="dx-bar">
                        <i data-score={d.score} />
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ---------- Close ---------- */}
      <div className="doc-page">
        <aside className="doc-margin" />
        <div className="doc-body">
          <section className="doc-close" data-rise>
            <h2>
              Run a case <em>of your own</em>
            </h2>
            <p>
              Enter a patient profile and review the terms, findings, sources, and next
              steps in one place.
            </p>
            <div className="doc-cta-row">
              {isLoggedIn ? (
                <button className="doc-cta" data-cursor="LAUNCH" onClick={onEnterWorkspace}>
                  Enter the console <ArrowRight size={17} />
                </button>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <button className="doc-cta" data-cursor="LAUNCH" onClick={onLogin}>
                    Enter the console <ArrowRight size={17} />
                  </button>
                  <button className="doc-cta-secondary" data-cursor="LAUNCH" onClick={onRegister}>
                    Create an account
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="doc-colophon">
              <span>Research prototype. Not a certified medical device.</span>
              <span>Neo4j Â· Pinecone Â· SageMaker Â· FastAPI</span>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\KnowledgeUploader.jsx =====

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { 
  UploadCloud, 
  FileText, 
  Database, 
  Trash2, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  RefreshCw, 
  Sparkles
} from 'lucide-react';
import { getApiUrl } from '../config';

export default function KnowledgeUploader() {
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([]);
  const [isLoadingDocs, setIsLoadingDocs] = useState(false);
  const [docError, setDocError] = useState(null);

  // File Upload states
  const [dragActive, setDragActive] = useState(false);
  const [file, setFile] = useState(null);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [uploadStatus, setUploadStatus] = useState('idle'); // 'idle' | 'uploading' | 'processing' | 'success' | 'error'
  const [uploadProgress, setUploadProgress] = useState(0);
  const [uploadError, setUploadError] = useState(null);
  const [detectedRoute, setDetectedRoute] = useState(null); // 'guideline' | 'structured' | null
  const [validationWarning, setValidationWarning] = useState(null);

  const getAuthHeaders = () => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem('medgraph_access_token');
    return token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {};
  };

  const fetchDocuments = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoadingDocs(true);
    setDocError(null);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(getApiUrl('/knowledge/documents'), {
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to retrieve indexed documents list.');
      }
      const data = await response.json();
      setDocuments(data);
    } catch (error) {
      setDocError(error.message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoadingDocs(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => fetchDocuments(), 0);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [fetchDocuments]);

  const handleDrag = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.type === "dragenter" || e.type === "dragover") {
      setDragActive(true);
    } else if (e.type === "dragleave") {
      setDragActive(false);
    }
  };

  const validateCsvHeader = (selectedFile) => {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (e) => {
        try {
          const text = e.target.result;
          const firstLine = text.split('\n')[0].toLowerCase();
          const headers = firstLine.split(',').map(h => h.trim());
          const required = ['drug1', 'drug2', 'severity', 'effect'];
          const missing = required.filter(r => !headers.includes(r));
          if (missing.length > 0) {
            resolve({
              valid: false,
              warning: `CSV lacks required column headers: ${missing.join(', ')}`
            });
          } else {
            resolve({ valid: true });
          }
        } catch {
          resolve({ valid: false, warning: "Unable to parse CSV structure." });
        }
      };
      // Read first 2KB of CSV
      reader.readAsText(selectedFile.slice(0, 2048));
    });
  };

  const handleFileChange = async (selectedFile) => {
    if (!selectedFile) return;
    setFile(selectedFile);
    setTitle(selectedFile.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "")); // Strip extension for default title
    setUploadStatus('idle');
    setUploadProgress(0);
    setUploadError(null);
    setValidationWarning(null);

    const ext = selectedFile.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
    if (ext === 'pdf' || ext === 'txt') {
      setDetectedRoute('guideline');
    } else if (ext === 'csv') {
      setDetectedRoute('structured');
      const csvCheck = await validateCsvHeader(selectedFile);
      if (!csvCheck.valid) {
        setValidationWarning(csvCheck.warning);
      }
    } else {
      setDetectedRoute(null);
      setUploadError("Unsupported file type. Please upload a .pdf, .txt, or .csv file.");
    }
  };

  const handleDrop = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setDragActive(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      await handleFileChange(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleUploadSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!file || !detectedRoute) return;

    setUploadStatus('uploading');
    setUploadProgress(0);
    setUploadError(null);

    const formData = new FormData();
    let uploadUrl = '';

    if (detectedRoute === 'guideline') {
      formData.append('file', file);
      if (title.trim()) {
        formData.append('title', title.trim());
      }
      uploadUrl = getApiUrl('/knowledge/upload/guidelines');
    } else if (detectedRoute === 'structured') {
      formData.append('file', file);
      uploadUrl = getApiUrl('/knowledge/upload/structured');
    }

    const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
    xhr.open('POST', uploadUrl, true);
    const token = localStorage.getItem('medgraph_access_token');
    if (token) {
      xhr.setRequestHeader('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    }

    // Track upload progress
    xhr.upload.onprogress = (event) => {
      if (event.lengthComputable) {
        const percent = Math.round((event.loaded / event.total) * 100);
        setUploadProgress(percent);
        if (percent === 100) {
          setUploadStatus('processing');
        }
      }
    };

    xhr.onload = () => {
      if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
        setUploadStatus('success');
        setFile(null);
        setTitle('');
        setDetectedRoute(null);
        fetchDocuments(); // Refresh documents table
      } else {
        setUploadStatus('error');
        try {
          const res = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);
          setUploadError(res.detail || "Server failed to process document.");
        } catch {
          setUploadError("An error occurred during file ingestion.");
        }
      }
    };

    xhr.onerror = () => {
      setUploadStatus('error');
      setUploadError("Network connection error. Check if backend is running.");
    };

    xhr.send(formData);
  };

  const handleDeleteDoc = async (docId) => {
    if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to delete this custom guideline? This will remove all associated vector embeddings from semantic RAG index.")) {
      return;
    }
    
    try {
      const response = await fetch(getApiUrl(`/knowledge/documents/${docId}`), {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: getAuthHeaders()
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to delete the document.');
      }
      fetchDocuments();
    } catch (err) {
      alert(`Delete failed: ${err.message}`);
    }
  };

  const formatDateTime = (isoString) => {
    if (!isoString) return '';
    const date = new Date(isoString);
    return date.toLocaleString();
  };

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px', width: '100%' }}>
      {/* Upload Zone Panel */}
      <div className="clinical-card" style={{ padding: '28px' }}>
        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', marginBottom: '6px' }}>
          <Sparkles size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-cyan)' }} />
          Add documents
        </h3>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginBottom: '24px', lineHeight: '1.4' }}>
          Add a PDF or TXT source, or a CSV with medicine interactions.
        </p>

        <form onSubmit={handleUploadSubmit} onDragEnter={handleDrag}>
          {/* Drag and Drop Zone */}
          <div 
            className={`file-drop-zone ${dragActive ? 'drag-active' : ''} ${file ? 'file-selected' : ''}`}
            onDragEnter={handleDrag}
            onDragOver={handleDrag}
            onDragLeave={handleDrag}
            onDrop={handleDrop}
            onClick={() => document.getElementById('file-input-element').click()}
            style={{ minHeight: '180px' }}
          >
            <input 
              id="file-input-element" 
              type="file" 
              style={{ display: 'none' }} 
              accept=".pdf,.txt,.csv"
              onChange={(e) => handleFileChange(e.target.files[0])}
            />

            {!file ? (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px' }}>
                <div className="drop-icon-wrapper">
                  <UploadCloud size={26} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-cyan)' }} />
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>
                  Drag & drop clinical files or <span style={{ color: 'var(--accent-cyan)', textDecoration: 'underline', cursor: 'pointer' }}>browse system files</span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>
                  PDF / TXT for sources Â· CSV for medicine interactions
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '20px', width: '100%', maxWidth: '500px', margin: '0 auto', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
                <div className="drop-icon-wrapper" style={{ background: detectedRoute === 'guideline' ? 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.1)' : 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1)', border: detectedRoute === 'guideline' ? '1px solid rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.2)' : '1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2)' }}>
                  {detectedRoute === 'guideline' ? (
                    <FileText size={24} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-purple)' }} />
                  ) : (
                    <Database size={24} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-emerald)' }} />
                  )}
                </div>
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'left', flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                    {file.name}
                  </div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                    Size: <span style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>{(file.size / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB</span> | Pipeline: {' '}
                    {detectedRoute === 'guideline' ? (
                      <span className="badge badge-purple" style={{ fontSize: '10px', padding: '1px 6px' }}>Pinecone</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="badge badge-emerald" style={{ fontSize: '10px', padding: '1px 6px' }}>Neo4j</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Form details when file is loaded */}
          {file && detectedRoute && (
            <div style={{ marginTop: '24px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--panel-border)', paddingTop: '20px' }}>
              {detectedRoute === 'guideline' && (
                <div className="form-group" style={{ marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                  <label className="form-label">Guideline Citation Title (For differential analysis matching)</label>
                  <input 
                    type="text" 
                    className="form-input" 
                    placeholder="e.g. AHA/ACC Chest Pain Guidelines 2021"
                    value={title}
                    onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                    required
                  />
                </div>
              )}

              {validationWarning && (
                <div className="triage-banner warning" style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginBottom: '20px', padding: '12px 16px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '10px' }}>
                  <AlertTriangle size={16} style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: '2px' }} />
                  <div>
                    <strong style={{ display: 'block', marginBottom: '4px' }}>File Header Notice</strong>
                    {validationWarning}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '12px', justifyContent: 'flex-end' }}>
                <button 
                  type="button" 
                  className="btn btn-secondary" 
                  onClick={() => { setFile(null); setDetectedRoute(null); setValidationWarning(null); }}
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>
                <button 
                  type="submit" 
                  className="btn btn-primary"
                  disabled={uploadStatus === 'uploading' || uploadStatus === 'processing'}
                >
                  Upload file
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </form>

        {/* Ingest Progress Status */}
        {(uploadStatus === 'uploading' || uploadStatus === 'processing') && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: '24px', padding: '18px', background: 'rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.03)', border: '1px solid rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.15)', borderRadius: '10px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '8px' }}>
              <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-contrast)', fontWeight: '700' }}>
                {uploadStatus === 'uploading' ? "Uploading file..." : "Preparing file..."}
              </span>
              <span style={{ color: 'var(--accent-cyan)', fontWeight: '700', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>{uploadProgress}%</span>
            </div>
            <div className="confidence-slider-bg" style={{ height: '8px' }}>
              <div 
                className="confidence-slider-fill" 
                style={{ width: `${uploadProgress}%`, height: '8px', backgroundColor: 'var(--accent-cyan)' }}
              ></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {uploadStatus === 'success' && (
          <div className="triage-banner" style={{ background: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.05)', borderColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2)', color: 'var(--accent-emerald)', marginTop: '24px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', padding: '14px 18px', borderRadius: '10px' }}>
            <CheckCircle2 size={18} style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
            <div style={{ fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <strong>Upload complete:</strong> Source added.
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {uploadStatus === 'error' && (
          <div className="triage-banner critical" style={{ marginTop: '24px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: '12px', padding: '14px 18px', borderRadius: '10px' }}>
            <AlertTriangle size={18} style={{ flexShrink: 0, marginTop: '2px' }} />
            <div style={{ fontSize: '13px' }}>
              <strong>Upload failed:</strong> {uploadError}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Indexed Documents Table Panel */}
      <div className="clinical-card" style={{ padding: '28px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '20px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--panel-border)', paddingBottom: '12px' }}>
          <div>
            <h3 style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>Uploaded sources</h3>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: '2px' }}>Your uploaded files used in case searches.</p>
          </div>
          <button 
            className="btn btn-secondary btn-icon-only" 
            onClick={fetchDocuments}
            disabled={isLoadingDocs}
            title="Refresh Database Index"
            style={{ width: '32px', height: '32px' }}
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} className={isLoadingDocs ? 'spin-animation' : ''} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {isLoadingDocs ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', padding: '48px 0' }}>
            <RefreshCw size={26} className="spin-animation" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)' }}>Querying index namespace stats...</span>
          </div>
        ) : docError ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', color: 'var(--accent-rose)', fontSize: '13px', padding: '24px 0', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
            <AlertTriangle size={16} />
            Ingestion registry unreachable: {docError}
          </div>
        ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', padding: '48px 0', border: '1px dashed var(--panel-border)', borderRadius: '10px' }}>
            <FileText size={36} style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
            <div style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontWeight: '600' }}>No uploads yet</div>
            <div style={{ fontSize: '11.5px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>Uploaded files will be available in future case searches.</div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="history-table-container" style={{ margin: 0, maxHeight: '350px', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
            <table className="history-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th className="history-th" style={{ padding: '10px 16px', fontSize: '11px' }}>Citation Reference Title</th>
                  <th className="history-th" style={{ padding: '10px 16px', fontSize: '11px' }}>Ingested Filename</th>
                  <th className="history-th" style={{ padding: '10px 16px', fontSize: '11px', textAlign: 'center' }}>Vectors</th>
                  <th className="history-th" style={{ padding: '10px 16px', fontSize: '11px' }}>Ingestion Date</th>
                  <th className="history-th" style={{ padding: '10px 16px', fontSize: '11px', textAlign: 'right' }}>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {documents.map((doc) => (
                  <tr key={doc.document_id} className="history-tr">
                    <td className="history-td" style={{ padding: '12px 16px' }}>
                      <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>{doc.title}</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td className="history-td" style={{ padding: '12px 16px', fontSize: '12px' }}>
                      {doc.filename}
                    </td>
                    <td className="history-td" style={{ padding: '12px 16px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
                      <span className="badge badge-outline" style={{ fontSize: '10.5px', padding: '2px 8px', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>
                        {doc.chunks} chunks
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="history-td" style={{ padding: '12px 16px', fontSize: '12px', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>
                      {formatDateTime(doc.uploaded_at)}
                    </td>
                    <td className="history-td" style={{ padding: '12px 16px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                      <button 
                        className="btn btn-secondary" 
                        onClick={() => handleDeleteDoc(doc.document_id)}
                        title="Delete Guideline Vectors"
                        style={{ padding: '6px 12px', fontSize: '12.5px', color: 'var(--accent-rose)', borderColor: 'rgba(244, 63, 94, 0.2)' }}
                      >
                        <Trash2 size={13} style={{ marginRight: '4px' }} />
                        Purge Index
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\PageCurtain.jsx =====

import { forwardRef, useImperativeHandle, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import gsap from 'gsap';

const PageCurtain = forwardRef(function PageCurtain(_, ref) {
  const colsRef = useRef([]);
  const labelRef = useRef(null);
  const [label, setLabel] = useState('');

  useImperativeHandle(ref, () => ({
    transition(nextLabel, onMidpoint) {
      const prefersReduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
      if (prefersReduced) {
        if (typeof onMidpoint === 'function') onMidpoint();
        return;
      }

      setLabel((nextLabel || 'LOADING').toUpperCase());
      const cols = colsRef.current.filter(Boolean);
      const tl = gsap.timeline();

      tl.set(cols, { transformOrigin: 'bottom', scaleY: 0 })
        .to(cols, {
          scaleY: 1,
          duration: 0.48,
          stagger: 0.04,
          ease: 'expo.inOut',
        })
        .to(labelRef.current, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.16 }, '-=0.16')
        .add(() => {
          if (typeof onMidpoint === 'function') onMidpoint();
          window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'instant' });
        })
        .to(labelRef.current, { opacity: 0, duration: 0.16 }, '+=0.08')
        .set(cols, { transformOrigin: 'top' })
        .to(cols, {
          scaleY: 0,
          duration: 0.54,
          stagger: 0.04,
          ease: 'expo.inOut',
        });
    },
  }));

  return (
    <>
      <div className="mg-curtain" aria-hidden="true">
        {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className="mg-curtain-col"
            ref={(el) => {
              colsRef.current[i] = el;
            }}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="mg-curtain-label" ref={labelRef} aria-hidden="true">
        [ {label} ]
      </div>
    </>
  );
});

export default PageCurtain;

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\ParticleStream.jsx =====

import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';

/**
 * ParticleStream
 *
 * The connective tissue of the landing page: a braided ribbon of particles
 * that erupts from a vortex beneath the hero, flows the full height of the
 * document, weaves past the alternating sections, and converges into a single
 * node at the base.
 *
 * Implementation notes:
 *  - One fixed, viewport-sized canvas. Particles live in page space (y is a
 *    document offset) and are drawn at `y - scrollY`, so the stream reads as
 *    one continuous object rather than a per-section graphic.
 *  - The spine is a braid: three strands sharing a centreline, each phase
 *    shifted, so the ribbon appears to twist as it descends.
 *  - The centreline x is driven by the same alternating rhythm as the layout,
 *    so the stream naturally swings away from whichever side holds the text.
 *  - devicePixelRatio scaling, a single rAF loop, an IntersectionObserver to
 *    stop work when the page is not visible, and a hard stop under
 *    prefers-reduced-motion (which paints one static frame instead of
 *    leaving an empty canvas).
 */

const PALETTE = [
  [86, 137, 216],  // blue
  [138, 108, 200], // violet
  [212, 124, 176], // pink
  [78, 178, 172],  // teal
  [112, 145, 226], // periwinkle
];

const COUNT = 420;

export default function ParticleStream({ sideForY }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const stateRef = useRef({ particles: [], docH: 0, w: 0, h: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: true });
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');

    let raf = 0;
    let visible = true;
    let running = true;

    const S = stateRef.current;

    /* Centreline of the braid at a given document y.
       Returns x in CSS pixels. The swing mirrors the layout rhythm so the
       ribbon always drifts opposite the text column. */
    const centre = (docY) => {
      const w = S.w;
      const t = S.docH ? docY / S.docH : 0;

      /* Hero: tight column under the vortex. */
      if (t < 0.12) {
        const k = t / 0.12;
        return w * 0.5 + Math.sin(k * Math.PI) * w * 0.015;
      }

      /* Tail: pull back to centre for the convergence node. */
      if (t > 0.88) {
        const k = (t - 0.88) / 0.12;
        const from = sideForY ? sideForY(0.88) : 0.5;
        return w * (from + (0.5 - from) * easeInOut(k));
      }

      /* Body: swing between the free side of each section. */
      const target = sideForY ? sideForY(t) : 0.5;
      return w * target;
    };

    const easeInOut = (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2);

    const seed = () => {
      S.particles = Array.from({ length: COUNT }, (_, i) => {
        const colour = PALETTE[i % PALETTE.length];
        return {
          /* Progress along the stream, 0 at the vortex, 1 at the node. */
          p: Math.random(),
          /* Which strand of the braid, and where in its rotation. */
          strand: i % 3,
          phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
          /* Radial offset from the centreline, in px. */
          spread: 6 + Math.random() * 34,
          speed: 0.00018 + Math.random() * 0.00042,
          r: 0.85 + Math.random() * 2.2,
          colour,
          alpha: 0.34 + Math.random() * 0.6,
        };
      });
    };

    const resize = () => {
      const dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      S.w = window.innerWidth;
      S.h = window.innerHeight;
      S.docH = Math.max(
        document.body.scrollHeight,
        document.documentElement.scrollHeight,
      );
      canvas.width = Math.floor(S.w * dpr);
      canvas.height = Math.floor(S.h * dpr);
      canvas.style.width = `${S.w}px`;
      canvas.style.height = `${S.h}px`;
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };

    /* Draw one frame. `animate` false paints the static reduced-motion state. */
    const draw = (animate) => {
      const scrollY = window.scrollY || window.pageYOffset;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, S.w, S.h);

      /* Vortex bloom removed â€” the bright white disc clashed with the hero
         artwork on dark backgrounds. The particle braid carries the visual
         weight on its own. */

      for (const q of S.particles) {
        if (animate) {
          q.p += q.speed;
          if (q.p > 1) q.p -= 1;
        }

        const docY = q.p * S.docH;
        const y = docY - scrollY;

        /* Skip anything well outside the viewport. */
        if (y < -80 || y > S.h + 80) continue;

        const cx = centre(docY);

        /* Braid: each strand rotates around the centreline as it descends. */
        const twist = q.p * 34 + q.phase + (q.strand * Math.PI * 2) / 3;
        const t = q.p;

        /* The ribbon is tight at the vortex, widest mid page, tight again
           as it converges. */
        const envelope =
          t < 0.1
            ? t / 0.1
            : t > 0.9
              ? Math.max(0.06, (1 - t) / 0.1)
              : 1;

        const x = cx + Math.cos(twist) * q.spread * envelope;
        const depth = (Math.sin(twist) + 1) / 2; // 0 behind, 1 in front

        const [r, g2, b] = q.colour;
        const a = q.alpha * (0.35 + depth * 0.65) * (0.25 + envelope * 0.75);

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(x, y, q.r * (0.6 + depth * 0.7), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(${r},${g2},${b},${a})`;
        ctx.fill();
      }
    };

    const loop = () => {
      if (!running) return;
      if (visible) draw(true);
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    };

    const start = () => {
      if (reduce.matches) {
        resize();
        seed();
        draw(false);
        return;
      }
      resize();
      seed();
      loop();
    };

    /* Pause when the tab or the canvas is not visible. */
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        visible = entry.isIntersecting;
      },
      { threshold: 0 },
    );
    io.observe(canvas);

    const onVisibility = () => {
      visible = !document.hidden;
    };

    const onResize = () => {
      resize();
      if (reduce.matches) draw(false);
    };

    const onMotionChange = () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      running = true;
      start();
    };

    start();
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
    reduce.addEventListener('change', onMotionChange);

    /* The document grows as fonts load and sections settle. */
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => resize());
    ro.observe(document.body);

    return () => {
      running = false;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      io.disconnect();
      ro.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      reduce.removeEventListener('change', onMotionChange);
    };
  }, [sideForY]);

  return <canvas className="cine-stream" ref={canvasRef} aria-hidden="true" />;
}

                      <div>
                        <h4 className="triage-title" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-amber)' }}>No Relevant Evidence Found</h4>
                        <p className="triage-desc" style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginTop: '6px', fontSize: '13.5px', lineHeight: '1.5' }}>
                          {results.message}
                        </p>
                        <p className="triage-desc" style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: '6px', fontSize: '13.5px', lineHeight: '1.5' }}>
                          Add more specific symptoms, medications, or medical history and run the evaluation again.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : results.status === 'graph_unavailable' ? (
                    <div className="triage-banner warning" style={{ margin: 0, backgroundColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.06)', borderColor: 'rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.35)' }}>
                      <ShieldAlert className="triage-icon" size={26} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-amber)' }} />
                      <div>
                        <h4 className="triage-title" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-amber)' }}>Interaction check unavailable</h4>
                        <p className="triage-desc" style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginTop: '6px', fontSize: '13.5px', lineHeight: '1.5' }}>{results.message}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : results.interaction_check?.status === 'ok' && results.interaction_check.interactions?.length > 0 ? (
                    <div className="triage-banner critical" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                      <ShieldAlert className="triage-icon" size={26} />
                      <div>
                        <h4 className="triage-title" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-rose)' }}>Drug Interaction - High Severity</h4>
                        {results.interaction_check.interactions.map((interaction, index) => (
                          <p key={index} className="triage-desc" style={{ color: 'var(--text-contrast)', marginTop: '6px', fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: '500' }}>
                            <strong>{interaction.dr1}</strong> + <strong>{interaction.dr2}</strong>: {interaction.effect} (Severity: {interaction.severity || 'unspecified'}).
                          </p>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="triage-banner warning" style={{ margin: 0, backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.04)', borderColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.25)' }}>
                      <CheckCircle2 className="triage-icon" size={26} style={{ color: 'var(--accent-emerald)' }} />
                      <div>
                        <h4 className="triage-title" style={{ color: 'var(--accent-emerald)' }}>Safety Clearance Approved</h4>
                        <p className="triage-desc" style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginTop: '6px', fontSize: '13.5px', lineHeight: '1.5' }}>
                          No immediate pharmacological drug-drug interactions or severe warnings were flagged in your active lists.
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Advice cards */}
                  <div>
                    <span className="section-title">Safety Explanations & Advice</span>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '14px', marginTop: '12px' }}>
                      {results.diagnoses && results.diagnoses.map((diag, idx) => (
                        <div key={idx} className="diagnosis-card" style={{ padding: '18px', margin: 0 }}>
                          <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: '10px' }}>
                            <span style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>
                              {diag.name}
                            </span>
                            <span className="badge badge-outline" style={{ fontSize: '10px' }}>PATIENT ADVISORY</span>
                          </div>
                          <p style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', lineHeight: '1.6' }}>
                            {diag.evidence}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Care Checklist */}
                  <div>
                    <span className="section-title">My Recommended Care Checklist</span>
                    <div className="workup-grid" style={{ marginTop: '12px' }}>
                      {(results.recommended_workup || []).map((workup, wIdx) => {
                        const key = `rec-${wIdx}`;
                        return (
                          <div
                            key={key}
                            className={`workup-checkbox-card ${checkedWorkups[key] ? 'checked' : ''}`}
                            onClick={() => setCheckedWorkups(prev => ({ ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] }))}
                          >
                            <CheckCircle2 size={16} className="workup-icon" />
                            <span>{workup}</span>
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        </main>
      )}

      {/* Tab 2: Medical Visit History */}
      {patientActiveTab === 'visit-history' && (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px', width: '100%' }}>
          
          {/* Header Controls inside History */}
          <div className="clinical-card" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--panel-border)', paddingBottom: '16px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
              <div>
                <h3 style={{ fontSize: '18px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>My Medical Consultation History</h3>
                <p style={{ fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginTop: '4px' }}>
                  Archived clinical visit summaries, attending diagnoses, and clinical instructions.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Visit Search Bar */}
            <div className="history-search-wrapper" style={{ marginBottom: 0 }}>
              <Search size={16} style={{ position: 'absolute', left: '16px', top: '15px', color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
              <input 
                type="text" 
                className="history-search-input"
                placeholder="Search by attending doctor, specialty, diagnoses, or notes..."
                value={historySearch}
                onChange={(e) => setHistorySearch(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Visits Listing */}
          {filteredVisits.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="clinical-card" style={{ padding: '48px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
              <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: '14px' }}>No visit records matched your query parameters.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '16px' }}>
              {filteredVisits.map((visit) => (
                <div key={visit.id} className="clinical-card" style={{ padding: '24px' }}>
                  
                  {/* Card Header */}
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '12px', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--panel-border)', paddingBottom: '14px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '14px' }}>
                      <div style={{ width: '42px', height: '42px', borderRadius: '10px', background: 'rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.06)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', color: 'var(--accent-purple)', border: '1px solid rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.15)' }}>
                        <Stethoscope size={20} />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <h4 style={{ fontSize: '16px', fontWeight: '800', color: 'var(--text-contrast)' }}>{visit.doctorName}</h4>
                        <span className="badge badge-purple" style={{ fontSize: '10px', marginTop: '2px', padding: '1px 6px' }}>{visit.specialty}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>
                      <Calendar size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }} />
                      <span>{visit.date}</span>
                      <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)' }}>â€¢</span>
                      <span>ID: {visit.id}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Card Body Details */}
                  <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '24px', marginBottom: '20px' }}>
                    <div>
                      <div style={{ marginBottom: '14px' }}>
                        <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '10.5px', fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--text-muted)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.5px', marginBottom: '4px' }}>Reason for Consultation</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{visit.reason}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '10.5px', fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--text-muted)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.5px', marginBottom: '4px' }}>Documented Diagnoses</span>
                        <span style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{visit.diagnoses}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '10.5px', fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--text-muted)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.5px', marginBottom: '4px' }}>Prescribed Medication Plan</span>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '14px', color: 'var(--accent-cyan)', fontWeight: '700' }}>{visit.medicationsPrescribed}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Attending Notes */}
                  <div style={{ background: 'var(--input-bg)', border: '1px solid var(--panel-border)', borderRadius: '10px', padding: '16px' }}>
                    <span style={{ display: 'block', fontSize: '10.5px', fontWeight: '700', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.5px', marginBottom: '6px' }}>Physician Consultation Notes</span>
                    <p style={{ fontSize: '13.5px', color: 'var(--text-secondary)', lineHeight: '1.6', fontStyle: 'italic' }}>
                      "{visit.notes}"
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== C:\PROJECTS\MedGraph\medgraph\frontend\src\components\SmoothScrollProvider.jsx =====

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import Lenis from 'lenis';
import gsap from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

export default function SmoothScrollProvider({ children, routeKey }) {
  const fillRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const prefersReduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (prefersReduced) return;

    const lenis = new Lenis({
      duration: 1.15,
      easing: (t) => Math.min(1, 1.001 - Math.pow(2, -10 * t)),
      smoothWheel: true,
      touchMultiplier: 1.35,
    });

    lenis.on('scroll', (e) => {
      ScrollTrigger.update();
      if (fillRef.current) {
        fillRef.current.style.transform = `scaleY(${e.progress || 0})`;
      }
    });

    const tick = (time) => lenis.raf(time * 1000);
    gsap.ticker.add(tick);
    gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0);

    lenis.scrollTo(0, { immediate: true });
    const refreshId = requestAnimationFrame(() => ScrollTrigger.refresh());

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(refreshId);
      gsap.ticker.remove(tick);
      lenis.destroy();
    };
  }, [routeKey]);

  return (
    <div className="mg-public-shell">
      <div className="mg-progress-rail" aria-hidden="true">
        <div className="mg-progress-fill" ref={fillRef} />
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

  .info-pipeline > div { flex: 1 0 100%; }
  .info-pipeline > svg { transform: rotate(90deg); }
  .info-contact-form { padding: 23px; }
  .info-library-toolbar { align-items: flex-start; flex-direction: column; gap: 12px; }
  .info-lead { font-size: 15px; }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .info-page * { transition: none !important; }
}

const isTokenExpired = (token) => {
  if (!token) return true;
  try {
    const parts = token.split('.');
    if (parts.length < 2) return true;
    const base64Url = parts[1];
    const base64 = base64Url.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const jsonPayload = decodeURIComponent(
      atob(base64)
        .split('')
        .map((c) => '%' + ('00' + c.charCodeAt(0).toString(16)).slice(-2))
        .join('')
    );
    const payload = JSON.parse(jsonPayload);
    if (!payload.exp) return false;
    return (payload.exp * 1000) <= (Date.now() + 60000); // 60-second safety window
  } catch {
    return true;
  }
};

const getInitialHistoryForUser = (user) => (
  user?.role === 'medical_practitioner'
  && user?.email?.toLowerCase() === 'e.carter@medgraph.ai'
    ? INITIAL_HISTORY
    : []
);

const getPatientDisplayId = (user) => {
  if (!user?.sub) return 'PATIENT';
  return `PAT-${user.sub.replace(/^usr_/, '').slice(-8).toUpperCase()}`;
};

const SERVICE_LABELS = [
  { key: 'neo4j', label: 'Neo4j' },
  { key: 'pinecone', label: 'Pinecone' },
  { key: 'redis', label: 'Redis' },
  { key: 'sagemaker', label: 'SageMaker' },
];

const getServiceState = (value) => {
  const text = String(value || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!text) return 'checking';
  if (text.includes('not_configured')) return 'inactive';
  if (text.includes('unhealthy')) return 'offline';
  if (text.includes('mock')) return 'mock';
  if (text.includes('healthy')) return 'online';
  return 'offline';
};

function ServiceStatus({ label, value }) {
  const state = getServiceState(value);
  const stateLabels = {
    checking: 'checking',
    online: 'online',
    mock: 'mock',
    inactive: 'off',
    offline: 'offline',
  };

  return (
    <span className={`service-status service-status-${state}`} title={`${label}: ${value || 'checking'}`}>
      <i className="service-status-dot" />
      <span>{label}</span>
      <small>{stateLabels[state]}</small>
    </span>
  );
}

function App() {
  // Navigation & Page State
  const [view, setView] = useState(() => {
    const page = window.location.hash.slice(1);
    return PUBLIC_VIEWS.includes(page) ? page : 'landing';
  });
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState('login'); // 'login' | 'signup'
  const [isLoggedIn, setIsLoggedIn] = useState(false);
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState(null);
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState(() => localStorage.getItem('medgraph_theme') || 'light'); // 'dark' | 'light'
  const [role, setRole] = useState('doctor'); // 'doctor' | 'patient'
  const [authToken, setAuthToken] = useState('');
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState('');
  const [serviceHealth, setServiceHealth] = useState(null);
  const [practitioner, setPractitioner] = useState({
    name: 'Dr. Evelyn Carter, MD',
    npi: '1982736450',
    email: 'e.carter@medgraph.ai',
    clinic: 'Cardiovascular Research Center'
  });

  const [isGoogleSsoConfigured, setIsGoogleSsoConfigured] = useState(false);
  const roleRef = useRef(role);
  const curtainRef = useRef(null);
  const IMMERSIVE_VIEWS = ['landing', 'how', 'about', 'library', 'contact'];
  const isImmersivePublic = IMMERSIVE_VIEWS.includes(view);

  const navigatePublic = (nextView) => {
    if (nextView === view) return;
    if (curtainRef.current && IMMERSIVE_VIEWS.includes(nextView)) {
      curtainRef.current.transition(PAGE_TITLES[nextView] || nextView, () => {
        setView(nextView);
      });
    } else {
      setView(nextView);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    roleRef.current = role;
  }, [role]);

  const mapUiRoleToApiRole = (uiRole) => (
    uiRole === 'doctor' ? 'medical_practitioner' : 'patient'
  );

  const applyAuthenticatedSession = (data, navigateToDashboard = true) => {
    resetWorkspaceState(data.user);
    setAuthToken(data.access_token);
    setAuthError('');
    setCurrentUser(data.user);
    localStorage.setItem('medgraph_access_token', data.access_token);
    localStorage.setItem('medgraph_user', JSON.stringify(data.user));

    if (data.user.role === 'medical_practitioner') {
      setRole('doctor');
      setPractitioner({
        name: data.user.name || '',
        npi: data.user.npi || '',
        email: data.user.email || '',
        clinic: data.user.clinic || ''
      });
    } else {
      setRole('patient');
    }

    setIsLoggedIn(true);
    if (navigateToDashboard) {
      setView('dashboard');
    }
  };

  const ensureValidAuthToken = async () => {
    const stored = authToken || localStorage.getItem('medgraph_access_token');
    if (stored && !isTokenExpired(stored)) {
      return stored;
    }
    // Never silently authenticate as a shared demo account after a real
    // session expires. That can cross account boundaries in the same browser.
    clearAuthenticatedSession(true);
    return null;
  };

  // Restore session from localStorage on load
  useEffect(() => {
    let restoreTimer;
    const token = localStorage.getItem('medgraph_access_token');
    const storedUser = localStorage.getItem('medgraph_user');
    if (token && storedUser) {
      if (isTokenExpired(token)) {
        console.warn("Stored session token is expired. Clearing stale session.");
        localStorage.removeItem('medgraph_access_token');
        localStorage.removeItem('medgraph_user');
      } else {
        try {
          const parsedUser = JSON.parse(storedUser);
          restoreTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
            setAuthToken(token);
            setCurrentUser(parsedUser);
            setIsLoggedIn(true);
            setRole(parsedUser.role === 'medical_practitioner' ? 'doctor' : 'patient');
            setHistory(getInitialHistoryForUser(parsedUser));
            if (parsedUser.role === 'medical_practitioner') {
              setPractitioner({
                name: parsedUser.name || '',
